3-1(di): gya gar skad du/tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra ga ru Da tan tra ma hA
3-2(di): gu h+ya arba saM gra ha/bod skad du/gtum po chen po ma rungs pa dzo rdze khyad
3-3(di): dang bcas pa don 'dus pa gsang ba'i rgyad chen po'i don btus pa'o//dpal
3-4(di): phyag na rdzo rdze la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas yang dag bka' stsal pa/sbyir na 'gro
3-5(di): ba rigs drug las//las dang skal bas 'phags gyur pa'i//'gro ba sems
3-6(di): can mi rnams la//sa bdag klu yis zin gyur na//drug las grol ba'i rgyud
3-7(di): 'di bshad//rgyal rigs dang ni rje rigs dang //bram ze dmangs dang
3-8(di): gdol pa yang //du ga ni dbyung bar 'dod pa yang /rgyal rigs la yang lngar
3-9(di): bstan kyang //dug ni dzo bo bzhi yin te//de bzhin du rigs lnga la yang shes
3-10(di): par bya'o//de la rgyal rigs dang /rdze'u'i rigs dang /bram ze gsum la
3-11(di): drag po byas pa'am/sbyin sreg la sogs pa byas na slar gdug pa cher 'gyur te/
4-1(di): dmangs dang gdol pa la ni drag po kho na'o//zhes gsungs so//le'u dang
4-2(di): de nas yang bka' stsal pa/rgyal rigs sa'i dug can dbyung ba ni/oM
4-3(di): tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra ga ru Da hUM phaT/oM khro badzra ga ru ya sA hA/zhes bzlas
4-4(di): pas ka ba gzugs so//de nas sdod srog tshig gdags so//dang po snying rjes
4-5(di): klud dang gtor ma byin//phug tshol yag ne bcu bzhi pa'o//zhes gsungs so//
4-6(di): de nas yang bka' stsal pa/rdze'u rigs kyi dug dbyung ba ni/oM tsaHa ma
4-7(di): hA ro Sha Na badzra ga ru Da hUM phaT/nA garbud/nA gshig/nA ga tsit+ta la swA hA/
4-8(di): zhes pas ka ba btsugs so//ma dang spangs pa yod na de la bzlas pa dag byas
4-9(di): na/rmus nas 'gro'o//de nas 'di gdags so//sA hA gong du/nA ga byer
4-10(di): byer phaT phaT/ces pa dang /ha sa ha sa/te ba te paM/kri krung kri krang
4-11(di): nA gashan ting phaT phaT/ces pas nad grol bar bya'o//gal te zug skyes na/
4-12(di): ha sa bor la/badzra dz+wa la raM/zhes pa gdags so//dang po lta skom/gtor
4-13(di): ma sbyin/mchil ma gdags so//de nas sha ro 'am/mi bde ba de ma sos na/
4-14(di): srog tshig gab tsam drang /phug tshol yang 'dra'o//zhes gsungs so//le'u
4-15(di): de nas yang bka' stsal pa/bram ze'i rigs la ni/mo tsaNa ma thA ra Sha
5-1(di): kang dang bcas pa ngan 'drus pa gsang ba'i rtad chen pa'i dan bcus pa/
5-2(di): badzra ga ca Da hUM/ma khro badzra pA ni ga tsa Na na ba spar sha ya phaT/ces pas rkang
5-3(di): gzugs/srog tshig bzlas/de nas phug tshal bya'o//zhes gsungs so//
5-4(di): de nas yang daga bka' stsal pa/dmangs rigs la ni dang po rang nas/
5-5(di): oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra ga ru Da hUM phaT/srog tshig naM rbad/lus rdul nA ga
5-6(di): mA ra ya phaT/raM raM dz+a la dz+wa la shi shi rdul phun nA ga mA ra ya sod rbad/ces
5-7(di): bzlas la/de nas cung ma slong na/ag ni bcu bzhi pa bzlas/de na sa nA
5-8(di): ga ti g+ha mA ra ya tsit+ta dza la raM phaT phaT/ces pas phug tshol nas bstan na
5-9(di): mthon par bya'o//sbyin sreg dang mtha' bcul tshabs che na bya'o//zhes
5-10(di): de nas yang bka' stsal pa/gdol pa la ni dang po nas/oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro
5-11(di): Sha Na badzra ga ru Da hUM phaT/ces pas ka ba yen cig btsugs la/de nas srog
5-12(di): tshig dang /nA ga yag ne dang gsum sbel du drang ngo //de nas ma sos na ye
5-13(di): shes dug tu btang ba'i ting nge 'dzin sgom/sbyin sreg kyang bya/chu ser du che
5-14(di): na gud du dbyung ba'o//sngags byang du byas pa'i sngags ris bzlas la phug btsala/
5-15(di): de nas mnan pa dang /sbyin sreg dang /mtha' brtul yang bya'o//sgur por
5-16(di): song ba dang gong gi ni sla ba yin te/dang po nas tsaN+Da la/srog tshig ril po
5-17(di): rim par btags la bzlas so//phug tshol 'dra'o//zhes gsungs so//le
6-1(di): sbyir kru dang sa bdag ni/mig na ba dang kha yo bar 'ong ste/gang ltar
6-2(di): byas byang grol lo//mtsha sman la ni drag sngags mi bya bar btsos so//kru bdud
6-3(di): kru btsan la ni tsaN+Da dang /nag po shu shu sharti ba ku tsa swA hA/zhes pa'i sngags
6-4(di): byas la/gong du klud sbyin par bya'o//zhes gsungs so//le'u bdun
6-5(di): de nas yang bka' stsal pa/gru'i sdong zla dpe da kar nag po gnod na/
6-6(di): oM tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha Na badzra ga ru Da hUM phaT/gnod byed mA ra ya sad/dz+wa la raM
6-7(di): tsit+ta thum ril rbada ya/zhes pa dang /drag sngags thams cad kyis brdung ngo/
6-8(di): de nas man ngag bshad bya ba ni/sbyir sa bdag gso na/rang la bsrung
6-9(di): ba gsum gdags/bsnyen pa skyel na mi dgos/nad pa'i lce zlos ma shes na/
6-10(di): dzo rje rtse gsum par sgoms la/srog tshig bzlas/pho ba na zhing zas ma zhu na/
6-11(di): zas la yag ne bcu bzhi pa bzlas la bza'/lha sgoms la bzlas la bza'/
6-12(di): lha sgoms la bzlas pas grol na/nA ga le dum pa le gdags/'thibs chena
6-13(di): yul lta btcos/snga dro thams cad la gtor ma btang /skabs la lar lha
6-14(di): sgoms la nan tan bya/skabs la lar snga'i ting nge 'dzin skom/skabs la lar
6-15(di): mchil ma gadaba/skabs la lar mdos dang btcos kha bya/zhes gsungs so//
7-1(di): gzum pa chen pa ma changs parda rje zhung dang bcas pa dan 'dus pa bsang ba'i rkyang chen pa' dan btus pa
7-2(di): dpala phyag na dze rdze gsang ba don 'dus nas/bal po ba su d+ha ra dang
7-3(di): gu ru pad+mas dgongs pa'i don btus nas las rgyad du byas pa'o//don la man
7-4(di): ngag yin pas yig sngar byas pa yin te/nad pa grol ba'i man ngag go//
8-1(di): rgya gar skad du/shri dza ma b+ha la da ya kA ru Ni ka tar+ha nA ma/
8-2(di): bod skad du/da pa la gnod gnas thugs rje 'byung ba'i rgyud ces bya ba/
8-3(di): dpala phyag na dzo rdze la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis zos pa'i dus
8-4(di): gcig na/ye shes kyi gnod 'dzin yab yum/ri rab kyi byang phyogs rin po
8-5(di): che'i gzhal yas khang dgos 'dod 'byung ba'i gnas na bzhugs so//de'i tshe
8-6(di): bcom ldan 'das mgon po gnod 'dzin gyis/'khor gyi gnod sbyin rnams
8-7(di): zil gyis gnon pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//de'i tshe 'khor sa
8-8(di): la gnas pa'i gnod sbyin yab bcu drug dang 'jig rten gyi gnod sbyin yab
8-9(di): yum 'khor dang bcas pa rnams 'dus shing 'khod do//de'i tshe gnod sbyin gyi
8-10(di): rigs bye ba khrag khrig stong phrag du ma yang 'dus shing 'khod de/de dag rnams
8-11(di): rin po che la sogs pa dgos 'dod 'byung zhing 'byung ba'i phyag mtshan sna tshogs
9-1(di): dpal bnod gnas thabs ze 'byung ba'i rgyad ces bya
9-2(di): bsnams nasa rin po che'i char 'bebs/zhal nasa rang rang gi sngags zlos shing
9-3(di): rang rtags srog gi snying po mdongs gsol du phul nas/gtso bo la gdung ba'i
9-4(di): sems kyis 'di skad ces gsol lo//e ma ho/nor bu bzang po la sogs
9-5(di): pa'i//gnod sbyin btsun mo brgyad la sogs//nyid kyi 'khor du gtogs pa
9-6(di): yi//gnod sbyin stong phrag thams cad kun//gnod sbyin gtso bo'i bka' nyan
9-7(di): te//las kyi bya ba gang 'tshal ba//gtso bos bka' stsal bdag cag nyan//
9-8(di): gnod 'dzin gtso bos gsung du gsol/ces zhus so//de nas 'phags pa
9-9(di): gnod 'dzin gyis 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//nyon cig 'dus pa'i 'khor
9-10(di): tshogs rnams//las ngan spyad na dmyal bar ltung //dam tshig gnyan la
9-11(di): gnas pa dang /bde gshegs bka' lung gzhung bzhin du//bla med byang
9-12(di): chub bsgrub pa dang /gzhan don daga la brtson pa dang /sdom pa daga la
9-13(di): gnas pa dang //rnal 'byor dam pa de nyid la//bla med byang chub thob pa
9-14(di): dang //nad med bar chad zhi ba dang //tshe ring mis byams bar chad zhi//
9-15(di): bkra shis zas nor 'phel ba dang/bde ba'i bsam pa rdzogs par mdzod/
9-16(di): ces gsungs pa'o//de nas 'khor rnams kyis 'di skad ces gsol lo//bla
9-17(di): med byang chub sgrub pa dang /bkra shis bar chad med pa dang /bsam pa
9-18(di): yid bzhin bsgrub pa dang /gnod pa thams cad bzlog par bgyi//ces
9-19(di): da ma bcas so//yang bcom ldan 'da sa gnoda 'dzin gyis bka' stsal pa/
9-20(di): khyed kyi de ltar byas gyur na//sangs rgyas thams cad mnyes par 'gyur//ba
9-21(di): yi thub pa mar me mdzad//man d+ha ra bas mchod byas nas//byang chub mchog
9-22(di): tu sems bskyed nas//smon lam gya cher rab tu btab//ces gsungs so//
10-1(di): yang gsol pa/shin tu mdzes pa'i gnod sbyin gatsa//nyid kyi skye ba sngon rol
10-2(di): du//las su bya ba gang la spyad//gnod 'dzin 'phags pas bka' stsol cig
10-3(di): ces gsol pas/gnod sbyin chen pos bka' stsal pa//thugs rdze 'byung ba
10-4(di): zhes bya ba'i phyir//gsang ba'i bdag pos de la dris//gnod sbyin chen po
10-5(di): khyed rnams la//de yis mkhyen pas legs par gsungs//zhes bka' stsal

10-7(di): ba dri bar sems te stan la langs pa dang/gsang ba pa'i bdag po nyid kyis/

15-19(di): kun tu spang //me yugs nal la rab tu gzer//rta bong mi yi gsur mi
16-1(di): btang bsgrub khang thog tu gzhan mi btang //dngos grub thob par gyur
16-2(di): pa'i che//rdzas ni bzhi cha bzhi cu bso//cha gcig bla ma dkon mchog mchod/
16-3(di): cha gcig tchas skyong srung ma mchad//cha gcig 'gro la sbyin par btang /
16-4(di): gcig rnal 'byor bdag nyid spyad//mtshan dug bud med phyir mi btang /
16-5(di): de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba//bsnyen pa'i sngags ni 'di dag go//
16-6(di): thugs kyi snying po 'di bzlas so//oM dzam b+ha la dza len drA ya raHa sid+hi tsit+ta hUM/
16-7(di): 'di ni yon tan snying po yin//oM dzan+ta la dza len drA ya hUM hUM/'di ni nye ba'i
16-8(di): snying po yin//oM AHhUM dzan+dza la dza len drA ya/raN+na sid+hi pud+d+he ku ru hUM hUM/
16-9(di): 'di ni spyan drabs pa'i sngags so//e h+ye ma hA yak+Sha dzam b+ha la dza len d+hA ya
16-10(di): dzaHhUM baM ho/thugs bsnyen stong la yon tan khri//nye bsnyen 'bum la spyan
16-11(di): drangs lnga//bsnyen sngags kyi le'u ste bcu pa'o//
16-12(di): de nas gsang ba'i bdag po yis//nor zas la sogs 'khor rnams la/
16-13(di): 'dod yon lnga yi longs spyod rnams//dbang bsdu gter bca' snyan grags rnams//
16-14(di): nam mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam byed pa'i//de yi thabs mchog 'di dag bstan//
16-15(di): sngar bstan pa yi dkyil 'khor du//bkra shis bum pa mdzes pa bcu//mgul
16-16(di): ba dar gyi mdzes ldan cing //'bru sman dri snying rin chen snying //mngar gsum
16-17(di): dag gis rab tu dgang //kha rgyan dbus su lugs 'bur bris//sku gzugs
16-18(di): daga dang ne le yi//rgyal bur gong rdzas bkra shis pa//zas sna dbus su rnam
17-1(di): dpal gnang bnas thabs rje 'byung ba'i Dyab ces bya ba/
17-2(di): rgyal ra ba//bI dza pU ra gser mdog lnga//gzhan yang gser mdog dgu dag
17-3(di): la//rang sngags gser sogs bris la dril//da ra snas dril la nang du rtsal/
17-4(di): tsan dan byang bu legs pa cu/'dod yon lnga la longs spyad dang /
17-5(di): bsams phun sum tshogs pa yi//dngos grub chen po bdag la stsol/mi
17-6(di): sems bskul la nor rgyan khyug/ma dzam ha la dza len dra ya/rada na pu N+ye d+ha na
17-7(di): me ti na me thi na na la te da la saM bo ti sirta pa la yA h+uM dza/gzhan yang gang
17-8(di): 'dod bris la gzug/de nas phyag ma tshigs bdun gsum//yal gar bcas
17-9(di): pa rtse mo'i cha//rang mdog mthun pa'i bar dang bcas//gdags pa nyid du
17-10(di): bya ba yin//gong gsungs dngos grub rdzas mchod gtor//gtsang la bzang
17-11(di): mdzes rab tu bsham//de nas tshogs gnyis sngon song bas//gong du bshad
17-12(di): pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//gtso 'khor ma lus bsam pa ni//bI dza pu ra dag las
17-13(di): bskyed//rang bzhin gnas nas spyan drangs bstim//nyer spyod chu gtor sbyin
17-14(di): sreg mchod//de nas bdag nyid thugs kha nas//'od 'phros gtso 'khor
17-15(di): rnams la phog/mnyes pa'i mchod phul de dag las//'od 'phros bskul don
17-16(di): char phab cing //bcud bsdus rdzas rnams dag la bstim//ne le'i rin chen
17-17(di): gang gyur cing //de sa skyugs bdag gi gnas kun gang //de ltar bsam zhing
17-18(di): bzlas pa ni//gtso 'bum 'khor khri bzlas par bya//oM dzam+ta la dza len drA ya
17-19(di): swA hA/oM ba su d+ha ri ni swA hA/oM ma Ni b+ha dra ya swA hA/oM tsan dra ka ta ye swa
17-20(di): hA/oM puNa +ha dra ya sA hA/oM dan r+ha ye swA hA/oM r+ha na d+ha ye swA hA/maM su
17-21(di): dan ta ye sA hA/mo be shra ma Na ye swA hA/oM Ar+ya ya swA hA/oM pi tsi kuN+Da li
17-22(di): ye swA hA/oM su tha dra ya swA hA/oM ki li mi li ni swA hA/oM gu pa ta ye swA hA/
18-1(di): ma mu khen dra ye swA hA/ya da ba bya ye swA hA/maM tsa ran dra ye swA hA/mo sa ra
18-2(di): sa t+ye sA hA/de ltar rtsa sngags grangs rdzogs shing //rtags rnams mthong nas
18-3(di): las la sbyar//ngan pa'i rtags byung bar chad bsal//las sbyar sngags dang
18-4(di): dus bzhi ste//snga dro'i cha la sngags bzas pa//o dzam tha la dza len dA ya swA hA/
18-5(di): rany+dza puN+ye si d+hi pa la hUM a dza/gong gi mgo non kun la sbyar//d+ha na me d+hi sid+hi
18-6(di): pha la o hUM dzaHnana kha te sid+hi pha la a hUM dzaHd+ha la sa bo d+hi sid+ha pha la A hUM
18-7(di): dzaHdang po bsod nams gnyis pa nor//gsum pa zas dang bzhi pa 'khor/
18-8(di): snga dro phyi dro tho rangs kha//rim pa bzhin du bzlas pa ste//de bzhin rang rang
18-9(di): sngags la gdags//de nas dngos grub rdzas kun la//tshe dang bsod nams
18-10(di): la sogs bstim/bsgrub pa bdag nyid spyad par bya/gtor ma byang gi
18-11(di): phyogs su btang /rten rnams bang mdzod gter du sba//tshes brgyad la
18-12(di): sogs dus rnams la//skya yi rten rnams spyan drangs te//chu sbyin la sogs
18-13(di): rnams kyis mchod//'dod pa'i don la gsol ba gdaba//de ltar 'bad par
18-14(di): gyur pa na//mi bzad gter la spyod par 'gyur//snyan grags nam mkha'i
18-15(di): mtha' khyab gyur//tshe dang bsod nams rgyas shing 'phel//gzi brjid nyi ma'i
18-16(di): 'od 'dra zhing /bdud dang bdud kyi rigs rnams kyi//nam yang glags
18-17(di): ltar ga la nus/'jig rten gyi nor rdzas dbang du bsu zhing gter bca' ba
18-18(di): da ba snyan grags bsgrub pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o//
18-19(di): de nas rtags rnams bshad pa ni//dza sgra 'brug sngogs dung 'bud dang /
18-20(di): me tog 'thu dang rin chen rnyed//'bru dang nor char lo legs dang //'phral
19-1(di): du bza' ba ster ba rmi//shing gcod pa dang ri la 'dzeg/ra ba dang 'bring du
19-2(di): shes par bya//shing 'thu blangs pas zas ster dang /tha ma'i rtags su bshad
19-3(di): pa'o//mi lam gzhan gyis nor khyer dang //lo thog srin zhugs ser bas khyer//
19-4(di): shing la ha ba thob byed pa dang //gcer bu thur ban gtong ba ni//ngan pa'i
19-5(di): rtags su bshad pa yin//'jigs shing phya sabs khyer ba dang //lung stong
19-6(di): byin dang rta bong kya//snod stong mang po thogs pa ni//tshogs dang gtor mas
19-7(di): ma skongs rtags//ting 'dzin zang ltod shor ba yin//mchod gtor btang
19-8(di): zhing ting 'dzin bsar//bar chad bzlog pa'i las la 'bad//rtags kyi le'u
19-9(di): de na sa nor bu bzang po yis//gsang ba'i bdaga la 'di skad gsol/
19-10(di): 'di gtad gang zag ji lta bu//gsang ba'i bdag pos bka' stsal pa//bla ma
19-11(di): la gus bsgrub la brtson//theg chen snying rje che ba la//rgyud 'di rab tu sbyin
19-12(di): par bya//khebgs shing chags la khro ba dang //snying rje med cing khro la gdug/
19-13(di): gtong ba'i blo dang mi ldan zhing //bla ma spun la brtas pa yi//dam tshig med
19-14(di): pa'i mi dag la//skad cig tsam yang bstan mi bya//rten gyi gang zag gi le'u
19-15(di): de nas 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams kyis//gsang ba'i bdag la 'di skad
19-16(di): gsol//e ma ho dpal ldan dzo rdze 'dzin//chos nyid byang chub sems zhi ba/
19-17(di): yon tan mthar phyin rgyas pa'i skya//khams gsum ma lus dbang du sdud/
20-1(di): gdug pa ma lus 'dul mdzad cing //dngos grub rnam gnyis ra ba ster ba/
20-2(di): sangs rgyas kun gyis rab mangags pa'i//rgyal ba khyu mchog khyod la 'dud/
20-3(di): ces bstod nas rang rang gi gnas su dang ngo //thugs rje 'byung ba'i rgyud las/
20-4(di): 'khor gyis bstad cing rjes su yi rang ba'i le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o//
21-1(di): rgya gar skad du/sarba sid+hi gu h+ya ya kA ru no da yang shI dza ma b+ha la
21-2(di): nA ma/bod skad du/dpal gnod 'dzin gnas kyi bsgrub thabs thugs rje
21-3(di): 'byung ba'i rgyud ces bya ba/da pa la phyag na dze rjze la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad
21-4(di): bdag gis thos pa'i dusa gcig na/ye shes gnod 'dzin yab yum gnyis ri
21-5(di): rab kyi byang phyogs rin po che'i gzhal yas khang dgos 'dod spungs pa'i gnas
21-6(di): na bzhugs so//de'i tshe de'i dusa na bcom ldan 'das mgon po gnod 'dzin
21-7(di): gyis/'khor gyi gnod sbyin rnams zil gyis gnon pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms
21-8(di): par zhugs nas/gnod sbyin gyi sgo nas bkra shis shing zas nor 'phel ba bla na
21-9(di): med pa'i byang chub kyi 'bras bu la 'jug pa'i chos ston pa dang /don dam pa'i
21-10(di): lta ba la snyoms par zhugs so//de'i 'khor sa la gnas pa'i gnod sbyin pho mo
21-11(di): bcu drug dang /'jig rten gyi yab yum 'khor dang bcas pa rnams 'dus shing
22-1(di): 'khod do//mdora na gnod sbyin gyi rigs bye ba khrag khrig du ma 'dusa nas
22-2(di): bzhugs so//bzhugs par gyur to//de nasa gnod sbyin 'khor stong phrag du
22-3(di): ma dang bcas pa dag kyang /zhal nas rang rang gi sngags zlos shing rin po che du
22-4(di): ma char du 'bebs pa/phyag na dgos 'dod 'byung ba'i phyag mtshan sna tshogs
22-5(di): bsnams pa/longs spyod yid bzhin du 'byung ba de dag gis/rang rtags
22-6(di): srog gi snying po mdongs gsol du phul lo//de nas 'khor rnams gtso la shin tu
22-7(di): chags shing gdungs pa'i sems kyis 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma ho/nor
22-8(di): bu bzang po la sogs pa'i//gnod sbyin chen po brgyad la sogs//zla ba 'od
22-9(di): zer ma la sogs pa'i//gnod sbyin gtso mo brgyad la sogs pa'i//nyid kyi
22-10(di): 'khor du rtogs pa yi//gnod sbyin stong phrag thams cad kun//gnod sbyin
22-11(di): gtso bo'i bka' nyan cing //las kyi bya ba gang 'tshal ba//gtso bos bka'
22-12(di): stsal bdagnyan 'tshal//gnod 'dzin gtsa bos stsal du gsol//zhes zhus
22-13(di): so//bcom ldan 'das 'phags pa gnod 'dzin da pal gyis bka' stsal pa/
22-14(di): nyon cig 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs dpa la//las ngan byas na dmyal bar ltung
22-15(di): dam tshig gzyan la gnas pa dang //bde gshegs bka' lung gzhung bzhin du/
22-16(di): bla med byang chub sgrub pa dang //gzhan don dag la brtson pa dang //sdom pa
22-17(di): gsum la gnas pa yi//rnal 'byor dam pa de nyid la//bla med byang chub thob
22-18(di): pa dang //nada med bar chad zhi ba dang //tshe rings sris rme mi rnams byams/
22-19(di): bkra shis nor zas 'phel ba dang /bde ba'i bsam pa rdzogs par mdzod//
22-20(di): ces gsungs so//de nas 'khor rnams kyis skad 'thun par 'di skad ces

23-1(di): dpal bcang 'dzin gnas kyi btab cabs thabs rda 'dan ba'i bhyar ces bya ba
23-2(di): dang /bkra shis bar tshod med pa dang /bsam pa yid bzhin 'grub pa
23-3(di): dang //gnod pa thams cad bzlog bgyid do//zhes dam bcas so//yang

23-8(di): to//shin tu mdzes pa'i gnod sbyin tshogs//nyid kyis skye ba sngon trol du/

24-1(di): brtol shing gcig la//lha dang lta min rtsod pa byang lha rnams 'pham par
24-2(di): gyur pa las//rin chen nor bu lta yi bus//sangs rgyas mar me mdzad ces
24-3(di): la//me tog man d+ha ra ba gtor//spyan sngar smon lam 'di btab pas/
24-4(di): bcom ldan 'das kyis bdud rnams dang //bdud kyi ris kyi lha rnams dang /
24-5(di): lha ma yin thams cad 'dul ba dang rin chen gtera la longs spyod nas/
24-6(di): dbul po'i sdug bsngal sel ba dang //bla med byang chub thob par shog/de
24-7(di): skad smon lam btaba pa dang /sangs rgyas mar me mdzad chen gyis/de yi

24-9(di): po dzam d+ha la zhes pa//dgos 'dod dag la longs spyod pa'i//gnod sbyin du

24-11(di): 'phro can la dngos grub ster//de lta bu yi gnod sbyin du//skye bar de ni te
24-12(di): tshom med//phyag na dzo rjzes byin brlob cing //tshos dang 'thun par dbula
24-13(di): 'phongs sel//rin chen dzo rje'i bu ru skye//lta yi duga lnga zhi nas kyang /

25-1(di): dpal btnang 'dzin gnas kyi brdzub thabs thugs rje 'byung ba'i rtyad ces bya ba/
25-2(di): de nas 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams kyis/gsang ba'i bdag po la gsol pa/

25-13(di): mnyes byas nas//me tog sngon po dus chen mchod//skra gsung thugs kyi
25-14(di): rtags byung na//de nas gnod sbyin sgrub par bya'o//thugs rje 'byung ba'i

25-17(di): zas nor 'phel bas na//gnod 'dzin zhes ni de la bya//gser mdog 'dra

26-1(di): de nas 'khor gyi nor lha yis//gsang ba po la 'di skad gsol//mgon

26-7(di): mtsho chen po'am yang na dben pa'i sa//yang na bkra shis nyams dga' 'am/

26-11(di): spo ba dang ni ldan par bya//thugs rje 'byung ba'i rgyud las/gnas bstan

26-13(di): pa'i da la dang mchod pa'i rdzas//gsang ba'i bdag pos bshad par 'tshal/

26-15(di): theg chen byang chub sems ldan pas//lo zla gza' sar shis pa la/dga'
26-16(di): ba'i tshal lam kun dga' ra ba'am//yang na bkra shis dben gnas su//
26-17(di): bsod nams ldan pa'i rgyal po'am//khyim bdag bram ze phun sum tshogs/
26-18(di): khang khyim dag nas sa blangs pa//dkyil 'khor gru bzhi 'tshams par brtsigs/
27-1(di): dpal bcang 'dzin panas kyi bstab thabs thabgs rje 'byang baT nud ces
27-2(di): steng du rin chen sna tshogs brdar//gtsang sbha byi dor legs byas la//de
27-3(di): stengs pa blas ser por byugs//tshangs thig zur thig mtha' thig gdab/
27-4(di): thig rnams mkhas pas rdzogs byas la//de nas dkyil 'khor bzhengs pa ni/
27-5(di): pho brang 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad ser//rtsibs brgyad gang ras gser mdag dza/
27-6(di): mu khyad rin chen sna tshogs bri//gru chad ljang khung rin po che//bar khyams
27-7(di): phyogs tshon dag tu'o//pany+tsa ra gartsig par bya//de stengs tsan dan byang bu
27-8(di): la//dngos grub gang 'dod gsol byang bri//rdzogs par bris la steng du
27-9(di): bzhag/man 'ji chen po gcig gi khar//rul sbal khob pa yod na raba/
27-10(di): med na rin chen snod nang du//na sa dang gro dang 'bras la ni//phye byas gtor
27-11(di): ma bzang byas te//dbus su lha bshos bcug byas te//mtheb kyu dag gis
27-12(di): mtha' ma bskor//dkar gsum mngar gsum brgyan byas la//dkyil 'khor
27-13(di): dbus su dgod par bya//phyi yi mchod pa lnga rnams bsham//da ra ser dag
27-14(di): gis rnam brgyan bya//me tog ser po dus med mchod//mun nag mi shis mar
27-15(di): me rten//phud dang bdud rtsi rnam lnga bsham//gnod sbyin sbrul phyir spos dud

27-20(di): tshogs ji ltar bsgom//zhes sbas so//gsang ba'i bdag pos 'di skad
28-1(di): bshad//'dus pa'i tshogs chen kun nyon cig/phyi rabs don du bshad kyis

28-19(di): 'ong bsam pa 'grub la tchos dang 'phrad//bsrung ba'i mchog tu 'di
29-1(di): dpal gnar 'dzin gnas bya bathab thabs zhags the 'byang ba'i thad ces za ba/
29-2(di): 'gyur ra/thugs rje 'byung ba'i rtyad las/ba da gzung ba'i le'u te bdun

29-4(di): ltar lags//zhes dris pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad gsungs//
29-5(di): nyon cig 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs kun//lta ba snang stong dbyer med la/
29-6(di): yengs med stong par sang de zhog/sgom pa gsal stong 'dzin med la//rang
29-7(di): 'byung ye shes sa le bsgom//spyod pa rang byang lcag pa yin//rang rgyad bzang

29-9(di): 'byung //phyi rabs don du bshad byas pas//lung bstan dag pas dpal gyi
29-10(di): dgongs pa ni//nam mkha'i dbyibs ltar ma g.yos dbyings dang kha dog med//
29-11(di): rgyal ba'i yum mchog rtogs pa kun dang bral zhing 'das/chos dbyings nyon

29-14(di): dgongs pa yin//theg chen byang chub sangs rgyas bla na med//'di la ye shes
29-15(di): sbyor lam rnal 'byor kyang /sku gsum thig le zhag gcig ngang la ma g.yos

29-20(di): 'byung ba'i rgyad las/lha sgom spyod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o//
30-1(di): bcom ldan 'das kyis yang gsungs pa/nyon cig 'khor du gtogs pa
30-2(di): rnams//dam tshig spyod pa 'di lta ste//gsang spyod dkyil 'khor dbang rnams

31-1(di): dpal gnad 'dzin banas kyi brtab thabs thugs ze 'byang ba'i cud ces

31-14(di): grangs shes pa la//stegs bu gang bzhin brtsigs byas la//'khor lo rtsibs
31-15(di): brgyad nang du ni//dbus su ne'u le gcig gzhug go//rtsibs brgyad dag la
31-16(di): re re'o//dbus kyi ne'u le gcig nang du//ye shes nor lha yab yum gyi//
31-17(di): rtsa sngags gser gyis bris nas ni//shing thog rnam rgyal nang du gzhug/'jig

31-20(di): bya/ne'u le'i kha nas rin chen skrag par bya/re re dag la ye shes dgug
32-1(di): gzhug bya//de ltar raba gnas bzang byas la//rnam lnga la sogs pu tsa bsham/
32-2(di): gnod sbyin tshogs la dbul bar bya'o//yid bzhin da ngos grub ster bar bsam/
32-3(di): bdag nyid gsang bdag gsal ba yis//gnod sbyin srog sngags drangs par bya//

32-16(di): nas//gang 'dod dngos grub 'byung bar 'gyur//ne'u le'i kha nas rin chen
32-17(di): skyag par bsam//'od zer bde gshegs rnams la phog/mnyes pa'i pU tsa

33-1(di): dpal bnad 'dzin pa nas kyi brtab thabs zlabs rdze 'byung ba'i rhab ces bya b

34-1(di): cig pa'i//bris pa'i byang bu re re gzugs//phyogs bzhi mtshams brgyad

205-3(di): po che'i rgyad ces bya ba/gsang ba'i bdag po dzo rje 'dzin pa la phyag 'tshal lo//
205-4(di): 'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/ri rab kyi pha rol/ri gzya'
205-5(di): shing 'dzin gyi tshur 'gram/lcags ri nag po'i phar 'gram/du r khrod kyi ro sna
205-6(di): tshogs pa/me tog sna tshogs skyes pa/gzhi gser gyi sa gzhi'i steng na lcang lo
205-7(di): sna tshogs gtibs pa/gnam lcags kyi gzer btab pa/mi rgod kyi pags
205-8(di): pas g.yog pa/tshangs pa'i gdub byas pa/nyi zla 'char pa'i skar khung can
205-9(di): de'i nang na gnod sbyin zha 'on yab yum bzhugs so//de'i yab ni gnod sbyin
205-10(di): lag na gzhong thogs zhes bya ba/de'i ma ni gnod sbyin pad+ma bzang mo bya ba'o//
205-11(di): de'i 'khor du gnod sbyin dza l+ha la dang /lha chen ma hA kA la yab yum dang
206-1(di): lha chen 'brog gnas mched lnga dang lha chen rnam thos sras yab yum dang
206-2(di): kun tu 'jigs byed dang /gnod sbyin rnon po dang zhi ma'i mig dang
206-3(di): 'khor gnod sbyin stong sde dang lhan cig na bzhugs so//de'i tshe de'i dus na
206-4(di): gsang ba'i bdag pos sngags 'di gsungs so//oM badzra tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha na hUM
206-5(di): phaT/ces phyag g.yas pa'i rdzo rje lan gsum gsor nas/'jig rten gyi
206-6(di): khams thams cad g.yos so//'gul/rab tu 'gul/ldeg/rab tu ldeg
206-7(di): go//dbus mtho na mtha' dma'/mtha' mtho na dbus dma'/mtho dman
206-8(di): can du 'gyur ro//de'i tshe gnod sbyin 'khor bcas lus rab tu 'dar bar
206-9(di): gyur to//de'i tshe gsang ba'i bdag po la 'di ci'i rgyu ci'i rkyen lags/zhes
206-10(di): gsol pa dang /bka' stsal pa/khyed rnams ni mi'i srog dang da kor rnams la
206-11(di): dbang bas/de la sngags kyi sgra de spras pa yin gyis/da ma las ma 'da' zhig
206-12(di): ces bka' stsal to//gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i rgyad las/gleng gzhi'i
206-13(di): de nas zha 'on gyis gsol pa/kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//bdag
206-14(di): cag rnams kyi zas skal ni gang la bya/btung skal ni gang la bya/mu ti ga rin
206-15(di): chen gang du dbab//'bru yi phung po gang du srungs//rkang 'gra mi yi bsad
206-16(di): nams gang du srel/ces zhus pas/gsang ba'i bdag pos gsungs pa/
206-17(di): legs so zhus pa rab tu legs/khyed kyi zas sal ni gsang phyi dang dam rdzas la
206-18(di): gyis//btung skal du bdud rtsi dang tsit+ta'i khrag la gyis//rin po che'i char ni
206-19(di): rnal 'byor sgrub pa po la phob//ces bka' stsal to//yang gnod sbyin zha 'on
207-1(di): gtsar byin 'ar dza rdze bdrang 'dral byi gsang ba rin pa che'i bub ces bya ba/
207-2(di): gyis zhus pa/bdag cag bsnyen bsduba ji ltar bgyi//gsang ba'i bdag pos
207-3(di): bka' stsal pa/bsnyen pa'i dkyil 'khor zlum po la/phyi rim 'khor lo
207-4(di): rtsibs bzhis mdzes//gru chad khyams dang sgo bzhir ldan//'khor lo'i kha dag
207-5(di): lha dang mthun//dbus su zha 'on yab yum ni//skya mdog mthing nag zhal
207-6(di): gcig phyag gnyis pa//g.yas na nora bu rin chen bsnams//g.yon pa bdud
207-7(di): ci'i bum pa'o//rin chen rgyan ldan nor gyi lha//yum chen gnod sbyin kun
207-8(di): dug mo/sku mdog yab 'dra nor bu g.yas//g.yon na gter gyi ne le 'dzin//
207-9(di): sku la rin chen rgyan gyis sbras/shar du lha chen yab yum bskyed//zhal gcig
207-10(di): phyag gnyis skra mdog da kar//g.yas na rin chen g.yon na ga'u 'dzin//yum
207-11(di): yang yab 'dra g.yas bum pa'o//g.yon na ne le rin chen gyis sbas//lho ru
207-12(di): mgon po yab yum skya mdog gnag/g.yas pa bed dang lcag sbar g.yon/
207-13(di): yum ni bum par g.yon na ne'u le 'dzin/nub tu 'brog gnas zhal gsum
207-14(di): phyag drug ser/g.yas g.yon dang po rin chen sgrom bu 'dzin/bar ma
207-15(di): gser gyi 'khor lo sman chen 'dzin//tha ma shing thog sder bzang bsnams/
207-16(di): byang du rnam thos yab yum skru mdog ser//g.yas pa rin chen g.yon pa ne'u le
207-17(di): 'dzin//yum ni g.yas na bum pa g.yon na 'phreng ba 'dzin/de lta bu ru
207-18(di): bskyed par bya/dbus su gtor ma legs par bzhag/de bzhin phyogs
207-19(di): phyogs dag la bzhag/phan dang dar yang phyogs su bzhag/dbus su chang
207-20(di): phud ma nyams bzhag/gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i gyud las/dkyil
207-21(di): 'khor dang /rdzas dang /lha bskyed pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o//
208-1(di): de nasa zha 'on gyis/kye gsang ba'i bdag po/bdag cag gi gsang
208-2(di): ba'i snying po 'bul gyis/oM badzra rat+na tsit+ta ra ga ra ti/ma hA kro d+ha ar+ya dzan+ta
208-3(di): la hri da/ma hA kA la ha na ha na badzre na/yak+Sha de ba pany+tsa ra/ma hA be sha ma
208-4(di): Na hUM phaT swA hA/zhes ni snying po gsang ba 'di/dpa' bo nyid dang sgrub
208-5(di): pa'o//zhag bdun bar du rgyan du drabgs//'bru yi phung po ster ba dang /
208-6(di): rin chen gter rnams rted pa dang //rang gi slob dpon 'khor bcas dang /
208-7(di): lung pa dar gyis khebs pa dang //me tog 'bul dang lta phyag byed//mon naga
208-8(di): 'khor ston chos ston pa//de rnams grub rtags yin par 'dod//gnod sbyin
208-9(di): gsang ba rin po che'i rgyad las/srog snying dang rtags kyi le'u ste gsum
208-10(di): de nas gsang ba'i bdag po yis/zha 'on nyid kyi chu sbyin dbul ba 'di
208-11(di): gsungs so//dang po mdun du zangs gzhang nang /bruM las rin po che ye
208-12(di): snod//pad+ma 'dab ma bzhi pa la//zha 'on yab yum dbus su bskyed//slob
208-13(di): dpon dang ni gnas bzhi dang //bud med na chung chas bzang dang //shar du
208-14(di): dzar+b+ha la ni yab yum mo//nub tu 'brog gnas mched lnga bskyed/bum pa
208-15(di): bdud rtsi'i rdzing du bsam//oM AHhUM gsum gyi bdud rtsir bsam//de nas lha
208-16(di): la dbul ba ni//oM badzra Ar+ya dzap+ta la/ma hA kA la yaHya de ba/bai shra ma NA ya/
208-17(di): dzaN+Da la dza len drA ya swA hA/lan bdun bzlas la dbu la/lha rnams ma zhes
208-18(di): nas dngos grub blang /gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i rgyud las/chu sbyin
209-1(di): de nas zha 'on drag pos/rigs brgyad spel na 'di ltar bya//ma la khrus
209-2(di): dang gtsang sba bya//dara snga lnga la lta lnga bskyed//ma yi lus la rtag tu bcang /
209-3(di): zla ba re la phud gtor gtang //'o dod 'bod cing bgegs rnams gzir/
209-4(di): bsrung ba'i mdud pa dag kyang gdags//sri rnams thar nas gtang rag gtang /
209-5(di): ling ga gnan cing sngags rnams bzlas//gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i rgyuad
209-6(di): de nas zha 'on chen po yis//ser ba bsrung ba 'di gsungs so//rang
209-7(di): nyid zha 'on chen por bsgom//shar du lha chen yab yum bskyed//lho ru ma hA
209-8(di): kA la yab yum bsgom//nub tu 'brog gnas mched lnga bsgom//byang du rnam
209-9(di): zos yab yum bskyed//nam mkha' dar gyis khengs par bsam//oM badzra rat+nza
209-10(di): tsit+ta ra ga ma hA Ar+ya dzam+ha la ma hA kA la yaHa de ba be shra ma Na rak+Sha ser ba ur na
209-11(di): ma ni ye sA hA/oM dzama b+ha la dza len drA ya sA hA/ser ba bdud rtsi'i chu ru
209-12(di): bsam//gnod sbyin rin po che gsang ba'i rgyud las/ser ba bsrung ba'i le'u
209-13(di): de nas bla dzo bca' ba 'di gsungs so//shel dang byi ru mchong dang ni//
209-14(di): pa wang tshe long zha nye dang //da ra sna lnga dang seng ldeng ge'o//sngags ni mthing
209-15(di): shog gser gyis bri//de rnams lus la rtag tu bcabs//rang nyid phyag na dzo
209-16(di): rjer bsgom//oM badzra tsaN+Da ma hA ro Sha na hUM phaT/de sa ni 'gyur ldog byed mi
210-1(di): nus//gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i bhyud las/bla rdza bca' ba'i le'u ste
210-2(di): de nas zha 'on drag po yi//bsgrab pa po la d gra dar na/bsnyen pa gong
210-3(di): ma ji bzhin bya//zhag bdun bar du bsdub par bya//rdzas dana dkyil 'khor
210-4(di): gong bzhin bya/oM badzra rat+na tsit+ta ra ga ma hA Ar+ya dza b+ha la ma hA kA la yak+Sha de ba
210-5(di): be shra ma Na athu sha dza/de nas rtag tu bzlas pa bya//rtags ni dam nyams
210-6(di): 'ded pa dang //'brub khung nang du bcug pa'o//de nas las la sbyar bar
210-7(di): bya//ra sogs daga la ling ga bris//gong gi sngags la 'di yis brdeg/che
210-8(di): ge mo'i badzra snying gong tshad/kha la mu lo ha khri tra la zad/pu na pra ti zad/
210-9(di): mug rbad/ma la ga lci lce la rbad/ma ri kha ri ya rbad/nyungs da kar nyungs
210-10(di): na ga rdzas kyis brab//ling ga dag la phur pas gdab//nyin gsum mtshan
210-11(di): gsum phur pas gdaba//gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i rgyud las/gsad
210-12(di): de nas bsreg dang mnan pa 'di gsungs so//ling ga sman ma the re la de/
210-13(di): chu shing nam gla ba'i me sbar la/me nyid zha 'on 'khor bcas skyed//he raM
210-14(di): tanga raM hi raM dza la raM/da ma nyams da gra bgegs dza la raM/de'i ling ga bsreg
210-15(di): cing lha'i zhal du phul par bsam/tho'i sa dana thal ba la gnan pa bya'o//
210-16(di): glang gi zod par bcug nas ni//lha khang chen po'i them 'og mnan/gnod
210-17(di): sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i gyud las/manan bsreg gi le'u ste dgu
211-1(di): de nas tshe bsgrub pa 'di gsungs so//bum pa dz+yaM las gzhal yas khang du
211-2(di): bskyed//nang du d kar gsum mngar gsum chang yang blug/zha 'on dpon
211-3(di): g.yog nang du bskyed//oM badzra A yur khe b+h+yaM ya badzra a b+hi Shiny+tsa a/skya ni dzo rje
211-4(di): lta bu ste//gsung ni 'gag pa med pa'o//thugs ni 'gyur ba med pa'o//
211-5(di): bum pa'i dbang bskyur zha 'on thim//gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i rgyud
211-6(di): de nas gsang ba'i bdag po yis rgyud gtad pa 'di gsungs so//lus
211-7(di): thung gzi brjid che la ngam nag pa/bla ma snying bzhin 'khur zhing dam tshig mig
211-8(di): gi 'bras bzhin srungs//bsdub pa'i rdzas dang yo byad rtag tu 'chang
211-9(di): sngags dang ting 'dzin mi brjed rtag tu dran//kun la mi sgra rang gi snying bzhin
211-10(di): bcang //de lta bu yi slob bu la//rgyad dang ma bu tshang bar gtad//ces

211-13(di): gnod sbyin gsang ba rin po che'i rgyad rdzogs so//
212-1(di): gya gar skad du/thri ma hA kA la gu taM ha la gu ti ha la sa ri sa
212-2(di): la tan tra/bod skad du/mgon po nag po dug gi spu gri rtsa ba'i rgyud
212-3(di): dpala phyag na dzo rdze la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus
212-4(di): gcig na/bcom ldan 'das gsang ba'i bdag po dzo rdze gtum po nyid/dur
212-5(di): khrod chen po lcang lo can gyi gnas/gsang ba'i pho brang gnama lcags rngur mos
212-6(di): gtams pa'i gzhal yas na/byang chub sems d pa'i tshogs dang /khro bo
212-7(di): 'jigs byed rol po'i tshogs dang /lha dang mkha' 'gro 'jig rten skyong ba'i
212-8(di): tshogs dang /sha za rdgam pa dregs pa'i tshogs dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/
212-9(di): dregs pa can 'dul ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//de'i tshe rgyal
212-10(di): po rnam thos sras kyis stan las labs te/bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/
212-11(di): mthu stobs bdag po dzo dze'i ska//gsang sngags bka' yi 'khor lo la/
213-1(di): gcig bsrung ba'i dpal mgan te//ma hA kA la gtum chen skra//don gyi jo bo
213-2(di): ji ltar lags//gnyen po gang gis 'dul ba yin//gsang ba'i bdag pos
213-3(di): bshad par mdzod//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//kA la 'od 'bar
213-4(di): drag shul can//'bar byed 'jigs pa'i rgyal po 'di//dkyil 'khor kun gyi
213-5(di): srungs ma ste/dug gsum nad kyi sman pa yin//rig 'dzin rnams kyi srog
213-6(di): shing yin//rnal 'byor yongs kyi dgra bla ste//bdud 'dul rnams kyi dbang
213-7(di): phyag yin//dgongs pa mya ngan 'das mod kyang //sku nyid 'jig rten tshul
213-8(di): bzung nas//dam pa'i chos skyong bsrung ma mdzad//sangs rgyas legs pa'i bla
213-9(di): gros la//dzo rje snying pos lung bstan te//bstan pa de la mngon sabs rgyas//
213-10(di): gnyen po nga yis 'dul ba ste//'das pa'i dusa na sngon byung ba//rdzogs ldan
213-11(di): bskal pa 'dzam gling ltor//ma nor ma btsos de bzhin nyid//dngos po bzhi
213-12(di): la gnas bcas kyang //nam mkha'i sbrin bzhin rnal 'byor lam//dge slong
213-13(di): gdams pa'i don ma mthong //gsang sngags bka' bsgo log pa yis//dus
213-14(di): min tshe zad mnar med lhung //dmyal ba yi dwags kha 'khor gnas//ri rab
213-15(di): lho nub srin po'i gnas/lang ka pu ra'i lus blangs te//grong khyer brlag
213-16(di): pa'i dregs par 'gyur//lang ka mgrin bcu la sogs pa/gzugs khams
213-17(di): man chad dbang du bsdus//khams gsum 'khrugs pa'i bdud du 'gyur/
213-18(di): bde gshegs thugs rjes bka' bgros te//zhi ba'i bden pas ma thub ste/
213-19(di): stobgs pas nag po'i gnas yang stobs//khro bo de bzhin gshegs kun mdzad/
213-20(di): skra yi ngo bo nyid sprul pas//nag mo mthu chen ma thul te//de bzhin gsung gi
213-21(di): rnam 'phrul gyis/skyon pa drag po ma thul nas//dus gsum bde gshegs
214-1(di): thams cad kyis//las kyi khro bor dbang bskur ba//mthu stobs bdag po na
214-2(di): nyid la//nag po 'dul ba'i gnyen por mangags//sangs rgyas thugs rdze khro
214-3(di): bo yis//thabs kyi bye brag mang po yi//'khor rnams dbang bsdus nag po

215-1(di): bzhin lhun gyis grub pa yi//gdod nas dag pa'i chos nyid dang //skra dang

216-2(di): 'khor bskyed//bkra shis dag pa nang gi rdzas//gzungsa ma mtshan ldan sems
216-3(di): bskyed bshad//las gzhung dang sbyar tshogs kyi mchod//zhag grangs bdun du
216-4(di): bsnyen bsgrub brtsam//yol bas bar chad dzo rjze grang //da nga la dkyil 'khor
216-5(di): dbang bskur bu/bag chags rtog pa'i sgrib pa sbyang//de nas phyag rgya rgyan
216-6(di): dbang bskur//mgon po 'jigs pa'i sku ru 'gyur//phyag mtshan rdzogs
216-7(di): gtad lag na 'bar//de nas mchod pa dbul bar bya/dam rdzas dbang
216-8(di): phyug sa bdun non//de nas slob dpon gzungs ma sbyar//da kar dmar thig
216-9(di): le gsum blugs blang //gsang sngags 'khor lo man ngaga ste//gsang bdag

216-13(di): gsum ldan par//ston ka'i lo tog smin pa bzhin//'bras bu rang dbang
216-14(di): med par 'byung //dngos grub 'thob sgo dbang yin pas/bla ma'i byin

216-16(di): 'thob na zye bar 'grub//'khor lo zhes su gang gsungs pa//stobs chen rtsa

228-1(di): gcod spu gri'i gsang sngags las//gru gsum sbag ma'i nang rol du//thod

303-14(di): maN+Dal steng du bzhag la/oM ma hA kA la rag sha du bed ni dza/ling li su ra

405-19(di): dang dpal gyi lha mo ser mor gyur pa dang /da pal gyi lha mo dmar mor gyur

406-3(di): med//don dam spos bral kha dog gzugs kyang med//kun rdzob s+t+ya ma tsam du

406-11(di): rtog rnams//thams cad zhi bar gyur pa'i 'phrin las mdzod//sems nyid

406-13(di): don dam thos bral kha dog gzugs kyang med//kun rdzob +h+ya ma tsam du rang gi

406-17(di): mdzad ma//bdag gi rnal 'byor ting 'dzin 'di yi mthus//sngon 'das rgyal

406-20(di): mdzod//de bzhin nang gi nada dang dgra bgegs rnams//nang gi tshul gyis
406-21(di): bsod nams ye shes rnams//thams cad rgyas gyur cig pa'i 'phrin las mdzod//
407-1(di): rang sems dbang du gyur pa'i khyad par ni//sems nyid gud na med cing
407-2(di): sems kyang med/don dam spos bral kha dog gzugs kyang med//kun rdzob

518-1(di): mtshan nyid ji ltar lags//zhes zhus pas/de nas skyu mkhar de'i rtse la byon

529-14(di): gsum gdong la khrag gis bskus//tri shul ban dmar phyag na bsnams//mche
529-15(di): ba gtsigs shing srog snying 'byin//rdzas la bsngags shing ngar blud la//srin
529-16(di): mo bzhi dang sgrol ging bzhi//dmigs pa'i rtsa dang srog gcad nas//sha khrag
529-17(di): srog la gtor bar bsam//drag sngags nyi shu ming dang bcas//'bru bzhis
529-18(di): gcod cing sgrol bar byed//ming bcas 'bru brgyad zor du 'phang //'bru
529-19(di): bzhi mnan cing las mtha' brcul//drag po phyi yaM las su bshad//bskol
530-1(di): dri'i thod par 'khor lo bri//gsang thun brgyad po rim pas dgod//'brub
530-2(di): khung nang du ling ga gzhug rabs chad mgo 'am glang ru ru/thun rdzas

530-4(di): dgu pas mtshan mo braba//zer 'phang gnan pas las mtha' brtul//drag po
530-5(di): nang gi lugs su bshad//du r khrod ras sam rgya shog la/snying gzer dgu
530-6(di): pa'i 'khor lo bri/rog po lte ka ra dmigs pa la/khram byang bcu gsum
530-7(di): gdags par bya//snying gzer dgu pa'i 'khor lo bskor//thun sngags dgu pa
530-8(di): yis zar thun dbab/nyi shu ming btags phur pas gdab//ra bsgral tsitH
530-9(di): ling ga gzhug/'khor lo gtums te gdan khram bya//drag po gsang ba'i las
530-10(di): su bshad/'khor lo drag po'i snying gzer dgu ra rog khram byang dang
530-11(di): bcas la//ming po'i sngags dang snying gzer dgu//lto sngags spu gri nag po ni
530-12(di): yang gsang thugs kyi las su bshad//rtsibs brgyad 'khor lo lte 'og bcas//

530-14(di): po spogs pa'i las su bshad//ces gsungs so//srog ti ga nag mo khros pa'i
530-15(di): rgyud las/drag po 'phrin las kyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o//
530-16(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis srin mo khros pa'i drag sngags 'di gsungs so//
530-17(di): rtsa ba las dang bcas pa yi//yid bzhin 'phreng ba 'di lta bu//thugs kyi klong
530-18(di): nas bka' stsal pa//badzra ka r ma rag sha du ti tri tri dza dza/dgra bo'i srog la
530-19(di): thum ri li li b+h+yo/ha la ha la yaM yaM/khaM rbad subs mugs snying baM rbad/khraM ha
530-20(di): ri baM hu ra tri thrag thums/ra tri khaM d gra bo'i srog mA ra ya rbad/badzra ka ru na
531-1(di): rbad/snying khrag tshal pa rban /srog la hur thums rbad/phyogs mugs tsit+ta
531-2(di): bad/skyabs tsit+ta la rbad/tshal bu si na saM du zad/dgra bo'i srog la hur
531-3(di): thum rbad/rtsa la zang zang tsa/myags zad/dgra bo'i srog la mA ra ya zad/
531-4(di): de dag nang gi sngags te/thod pa 'khor lo de la brdeg/thrag shig ha la yaM
531-5(di): dgra bo tsit+ta gzer thums zad/raga mo kha lo baM ri la da gra bo'i tsit+ta gzer
531-6(di): thums rbad/ra ga mo tsit+ta breng shag dgra bo'i tsit+ta gzer thums rbad/rag mo
531-7(di): duna ti dgra bo'i tsit+ta gzer thums zad/rag mo rtsa tha srog la zreng shag dgra
531-8(di): po'i tsit+ta gzer thums rbad/tibs khaM shag du na dgra bo'i tsit+ta gzer thums
531-9(di): rbad/hri pa tsa shu la yaM dgra bo'i tsi+ta gzer thums rbad/tri ling baM rim dgra
531-10(di): bo'i tsit+ta gzer thums rbad/ra tri khaM tsit+ta shag dgra bo'i tsit+ta gzer thums rbad/
531-11(di): dgra bo'i srog thun phyogs b+h+yo/srog stam b+ha ya na na/badzra ka ra ma rag sha du tri
531-12(di): hri hri ma srog la breng breng hur thum dza dza/ya ma sa ma ya ma ma raga mo srog la
531-13(di): duna du na dza dza dza/ya ma kro d+ha rag mo ma ma trig nana/gsang ba snying gzer
531-14(di): gnad kyi sngags//'di dag 'khor lo dkrug pa'o//ra tri khraM dgra bo'i srog la
531-15(di): rbad/trag thums dgra bo'i srog la rbad/hu ra tri dgra bo'i srog la rbad/khaM
531-16(di): ha ri ba dgra bo'i srog la zad/snying ba dgra bo'i srog la zad/mhugs dgra
531-17(di): bo'i srog la rbad/subs dgra bo'i srog la rbad/khaM dgra bo'i srog la rbad
531-18(di): yaM yaM ha la ha la d gra bo'i srog la mA ra ya rbad/srog ti ga na ga mo gsang ba'i
531-19(di): sngags//dug gi spu gri gdug pa'i mda'//srin mo khros pa'i yang snying ste//
531-20(di): lha srin yongs kyi grong khyer yin//ces gsungs so//srog ti ga nag mo khros
531-21(di): pa'i rgyud las/drag po sngags kyi le'u ste lnga pa'o//
532-1(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis chu 'khyags khrag tu d bab pa'i las 'di
532-2(di): gsungs so//rabs chad thod par rdzas sbrubs la//dkyil 'khor gru gsum
532-3(di): dag tu dgod//drag po dkrags pa'i sngags kyang bri/ta hu dug lcum bu dang
532-4(di): ra khyi ma'i ra ga ta dang /'bru sna lnga sngo sna thar bar sbyar/bsnyen bsgrub rdzogs
532-5(di): par thun dgongs la/rta gzhug zor gyis da bab par bya//ha la ha la yaM yaM khaM
532-6(di): rbad/subs mugs snying baM rbad/khaM ha ri baM rbad/baM ha ri thrag thums rbad/
532-7(di): ra tri khaM mA ra ya kar phrom du hri la/sa duna du pho ba/dmar phro du hri la/lo
532-8(di): dur du pho ba/seng dur du po ba/rkyang dur du pho ba/tro la thra ma yaM la/thun
532-9(di): b+h+yo thun b+h+ya/naga mo na ga mo/thog mda' can b+h+ya/badzra a si byi ru b+h+yo dza/
532-10(di): pa na d+h+yo b+h+yo mu myur su myur/tsem tsem sa ma ya b+h+ya/na ga mo nag mo thog mda'
532-11(di): can b+ya/srag tiM ga nag mo srin mo khras pa'i gyad las/chu 'khyag dbab
532-12(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sgrub pa rtags kyi le'u 'di gsungs so//
532-13(di): gang zag dam tshig rab 'bring gis//mtshan ma rnam pa gsum du 'byung /
532-14(di): naga mo khro gnyer ral pa can//bzhin mi sdug la so tham byed//dkyil 'khor
532-15(di): dbus su me 'bar dang //rlung ser tshub ma thog ser 'byung //de byung dngos
532-16(di): grub blang bar bya//mi 'dod byung nas las la sbyar//grub rtags zhan na las
532-17(di): rnams spogs//tshe ldog byed na mnan la dabab//las mjug sngags rnams
532-18(di): mas bzlog bzlas//de yis las rnams grub par 'gyur//ra g.yas gsad
533-1(di): dang ri dwags shor//bla shing gcad dang mkhar bshig na//dgra bo'i kha thum
533-2(di): 'gum par 'gyur//dam rdzas tshogs kyis gtang rag gtang //srog ti g nag
533-3(di): mo srin mo khros pa'i rgyad las/grub rtags kyi le'u ste bdun pa'o//
533-4(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis ming po srin po nag po'i drag sngags 'di
533-5(di): gsungs so//maN+Dal 'brub khung gru gsum du//gyu rten drug gi ling ga gzhug
533-6(di): rtsibs bdun bdud kyi lto bar bri//ro ras shing shun dag la'o//nag po ka li
533-7(di): ba la b+ha shos//gri mda' mdunga la sogs pa ni//ling ga daga la gdab par
533-8(di): bya//ra rog dag la khram byang gdags//nag po bdud kyi drag sngags dang /
533-9(di): snying gzer dgu pa nyid mtshan bsnol//ga ro ni sha na shag rbad/tsa ra pa sha
533-10(di): na shag rbad/mug ga du na shag rbad/dza la pa tra na shag bad/'du na bu ti
533-11(di): na shag rbad/ram di pu ga na shag rbad/wa ga sha ling na shag zad/tril du
533-12(di): zad/sa du ra bad/ra shi shi rbad/ki ki bad/thri ling rbad/tsa shu zad/
533-13(di): srog gsol srog long /ga ta ya mo ga zad/drag sngags brabs shing ra bsgral
533-14(di): la//ra snying dag la gnan pa bya//khra ni dgra la zar du 'phang //las kyi
533-15(di): thus ka gsang bar bshad//srog tiga na ga mo srin mo khros pa'i rgyad las/
533-16(di): ming po bdud dang spel ba'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o//
533-17(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di skad ces gsungs so//phyi rabs rnal
533-18(di): 'byor chen po dag/nga yis ngan sngags 'di spyod na//rgyad lung spyi yi dbang
533-19(di): rnams blangs//khyad par ma mo'i dbang yang blang //bla ma bka' brgyad ma
534-1(di): chad pa'i//khyad par byin brlabs yod pa las//ma zhes pa'i yon pul dbang
534-2(di): yang nod//ra ga ta'i bum pas dbang yang bskur//ma mo bsgrubs shig lung
534-3(di): yang byin//bkra shis dbang du tshe dbang bskyur/bsgrub rdzas 'tshogs par
534-4(di): dbang bskur na//ci 'dod rdzas la rol par 'gyur//'phrin las drag po'i
534-5(di): dbang bskur na//thogs med las la 'byams par 'gyur/slob dpon ma mo
534-6(di): sbyar byas te//byang sems dbang bskur ci sbras nyan/ma mo bu la gtad pa
534-7(di): na//ma mo nyid dana dbyer med 'gyur//dbang thob bu desa sdom pa srun/
534-8(di): bla ma pa ma tag tu bkur/dzo dze spun rnams byams pas gdung lus kyi
534-9(di): chas dgu rala na ga gyon//sngags dang phyag rgya rgyan mi gcad//rnam par mi
534-10(di): rtog chos nyid la//rtag tu ma yengs yid la bya/yi dam mi gtan ma mo
534-11(di): gsang rtsa ba yan lag zhyi shu lnga/gsang sngags spyi las grags pas bsrung
534-12(di): srog ti ga nag mo srin mo khros pa'i rgyad las/dam tshig dbang gi le'u ste dgu
534-13(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/d pa la lha mo re ma ti la 'di skad gsungs
534-14(di): nyon cig lha mo re ma ti//ma mo rang bzhin shes par gyis//ma ni skye ba
534-15(di): med pa'i dbyings//thams cad 'byung ba'i mdzod chen mo//thams cad
534-16(di): 'byung bas mkha' klong yang //ma bcos lhun gyis rdzogs pa nas/ma skyes
534-17(di): mkha' rtsol sems dang bral/ma ni dag pa chen po'i gnas/mo ni 'gag
534-18(di): med ye shes te//'phrin las ma btsal chos nyid rol pa'i brgyan//ji ltar
534-19(di): sbrul kyang ma las g.yos pa med//ji ltar sna tshogs stong pa nyid la rol//se
535-1(di): 'gag med pa'i klong du mya ban 'das/ma ni sna tshogs snang ba'i rol par
535-2(di): snang snang tsam nyid na snang ba'i dngos po med//ma ni stong pa chen po'i ngo

535-17(di): dpal la mo ma ma'i srog ta ga nag mo khras pa'i kyud ces bya ba rdzogs sa
536-1(di): rgya gar skad du/ba tharba nas zhag sam/bod skad du/shi

537-1(di): tan pa dpal srab ga 'khor tsho'i rdzom pa'i b+had ces bya ba/

537-20(di): pa'i rdzas dang ldan par bsam//sbrul gyi chun pos phyag zhabs gdu ga gur
538-1(di): brgyan//thod pa skam rlon tshar gsum da bu yi rgyan//de yi sku la ma mo rnams/

539-1(di): dam tshig bstan pa/rtsa ba sku gsung thugs kyi dam tshig ni//spyi'i grogs rdzas

539-14(di): khyad par can btang ba dang dam tshig gi skabs te le'u gsum pa'o//
539-15(di): de nasa man ngag khyad par can gyi dam tshig 'di gsungs so//ru nyal ma
539-16(di): nor rtsa ba'i sngags//de la bod yig lnga yi sel//tsa cha dza zha za/zhugs par

539-18(di): gi snying po la//de lnga'i sel zhugs 'khor la gzan//skra yi dam tshig nyams
539-19(di): pa'o//yi ge 'khrul dang bla 'og nor//blang dora dag tu gyur pa ni//gsung
539-20(di): gi dam tshig nyams pa'o//man nag zab mo'i don ma rtogs//sbyor ba drag
540-1(di): po'i 'khrul pas ni//de dog med de dam tshig nyams//de phyir man ngag zab
540-2(di): mo'i don//rgyad lung man ngag yig chung brtag/ces man bag khyad par can
540-3(di): gyi dam tshig bstan pa'i skabs te le'u bzhi pa'o//
540-4(di): de nas lta ba khyad par can bstan pa 'di gsungs so//lta ba theg chen yang
540-5(di): dag ni//som nyi med na kun gyis bgrod//theg pa'i don dang mi 'gal bar/

540-7(di): thabs mchog 'bras bu bla med don//bsgral zhes bya ba'i tha snyad med//lta ba
540-8(di): de nyid dam tshig ste//thabs dang shes rab 'brel ba'i don//de nyid dam
540-9(di): tshig yin par bshad//lta ba khyad par can gyi dam tshig bstan pa'i skabs te
540-10(di): de nas spyod pa'i khyad par bstan pa ni/ma dang sring mo byi mo la/brtson
540-11(di): 'grus sbyor ba'i brtul zhugs dang /le lo btang snyoms mi byed dang /
540-12(di): gzhung dang 'thun par lus kyi spyod pa byed//gzhi 'thun ro la dga' ba'i spyod
540-13(di): pa dang //gtsang sme med par dam rdzas lnga la spyod//rang gi bud med ma yin
540-14(di): pa//gzhan du 'dod log spyod mi bya//spyod pa'i dam tshig yin par bshad/

552-20(di): sngon mo dang /'gugs ma dang /sdom ma la sogs pa bsam gyis mi khyab cing

553-1(di): ral pa gcig 'grengs kyis stong gsum khyab pa/mig gcig gis stong gsum
553-2(di): gzigs pa/so gcig gis sems can thams cad kyi skye shi'i rtsa ba gtsod pa/
553-3(di): gshog pa gcig gis stong gsum thams cad g.yeng bar mdzad pa/rkang pa
553-4(di): gcig gis khams gsum thams cad khyab par 'gro ba/nu ma gcig gis sems
553-5(di): can thams cad kyi sri'u gso ba/'khor dmar mo'i tshogs dang /'gugs
553-6(di): ma'i tshogs dang /sdom ma'i tshogs bsam gyis mi khyab cing brjod kyis mi lang
553-7(di): ba byung bar gyur to//byang phyogs kyi 'od zer las dbang po thams cad la
553-8(di): dbang byed pa'i bdaga mo/skad cig gcig gis kun tu rgyug pa/mig la
553-9(di): sogs pa kun thogs pa med par 'dug par byed pa/de'i 'khor ser mo'i
553-10(di): tshogs dang /'gugs mo dang /'bebs ma'i tshogs bsam gyis mi khyab cing
553-11(di): brjod kyis mi lang ba byung bar gyur to//de ltar byung basa da pa la khrag 'thung
553-12(di): 'khor de da ga dang bcas te/phung byed ma de daga rnams da da pa las/d kar
553-13(di): mo'i tshogs de dag thams cad kyis/phung byed ma de dag gi dbugs la dbang
553-14(di): byas/rkun ma de dag gis bla dbang du bsdus/'gugs mas dkrug nas/
553-15(di): d pala chen po'i zhabs 'og tu bsgyur ro/dpa la khrag 'thung chen pos me chen
553-16(di): po phyogs bzhi nas sbar te/phyag drug gis mtshan chas gzir ba las thugs rje'i
553-17(di): bdag mo thong /bdag cag gzhan yang sngon sems can gyi dusa na dur khrod kyi
553-18(di): baM snying dang /bya'i snying dang /khyi shel mig po'i snying dang /lug kham
553-19(di): brgya'i snying rnams zas su btsal bas srog la dga' bar gyur te/de'i las kyi
553-20(di): rnam par smin pas sems can dmyal ba'i du kha yang nyams su myong ngo //de ltar
553-21(di): yang gsod dam ci zong /zhes brdzad pas/dpal khrag 'thung gis ha ha'i sgra
554-1(di): drag po bsgrags pas de dag brgyal bar gyur to//brgyal ba sangs nas 'khor
554-2(di): daga da pa la khrag 'thung gi 'khor 'bangs bgyid/srog gi snying po yang 'bul
554-3(di): bar 'tshal lo//srog gi snying po phul pa/tra ga rag+ha pa tra ru lu ru lu snying kha
554-4(di): rag ma b+h+ya/thrag rag+ha trag rag+ha e ka dza ti mu tra snying kha rag ma b+h+ya/ru ru ro ru
554-5(di): tsa pa la snying kha ra ga ma b+h+yo/ta d+ya thA ru ru ro ru bi ti ta pa to rmi ba to si b+h+yo/
554-6(di): zhes rang gi 'khor thams cad dana dam tshig khas len par gyur to//da pa la
554-7(di): chen po bdag cag rnams kyis dpa' bo chen po'i 'khor thams cad bsrung zhing
554-8(di): bskyang bar bgyi'o//dam pa'i shos tshul bzhin du bsrung ba dang bskyang bar
554-9(di): bgyi'o//bdag cag gi bza' ba'i skal ba gang gis bgyi/bka' stsal du
554-10(di): gsol/zhes zhus pa la/da pa la khrag 'thung gis gdana gyi 'og nas bton
554-11(di): na sa mna' bsgrags pa/bdud rtsi rnam pa lnga la/oM a mr-i ta tsit+ta tiSh+Ta/zhes
554-12(di): bdud rtsi blud de mna' bsgrags so//khyed rnams kyi bza' ba'i skal ba ni sangs
554-13(di): rgyas kyi bstan pa bshig par byed pa/da kon mchog gi da bu 'phangs smod par
554-14(di): byed pa/ngan pa'i ngang tshul can dben ris dang tshos 'khor bshig cing skye bo
554-15(di): yongs la 'tshe ba/de dag gi snying zas skal byed cing bsngos nas 'jig rten gyi
554-16(di): khams bde ba la bkod do//bzang mo stobs kyi srog gi rgyud las/gleng
554-17(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//sngon gyi las mi dge
554-18(di): ba las grub pa'i lo ka phung bar byed pa'i ma mo 'di dag/dpala chen po'i dam
554-19(di): la gnas par mdzad pa'o//'di ma 'ongs pa'i rnal 'byor pa rnams kyi bstan
555-1(di): pa bsrung ba'i slad du ji ltar bsgrub par bgyi/'di'i las kyi rnam pa gang dang
555-2(di): gang bsgrub pa bka' stsal du gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal
555-3(di): pa/nyon cig sprul pa'i mkha' 'gro ma/ma 'ongs pa'i don du sgrub pa'i rim
555-4(di): pa dang /las kyi rim pa 'di dri ba legs so legs so//khyod kyis dris pa'i
555-5(di): don de da ga ngas legs par bya'o//khams gsum du gnas pa 'di da ga ni/
555-6(di): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bsgral ba'i zhing yin te/rnam par rtog pa
555-7(di): thams cad gzhom pa'i phyir dang /pa la pa tra sgrub pa'i gnas btsal bar bya'o//
555-8(di): gnas yid du 'ong ba ni dur khrod chen po'i dkyil 'khor ram/gcan gzan
555-9(di): mang po rtse ba'i gnas sam/rgyan tu sems can gsod pa'i gnas de lta bur
555-10(di): bsgrub par bya'o//de ra sa brtag pa dang /gzung ba dang /sa'i cho lnga
555-11(di): dbyung ba dang /de ra ma mo'i sbyin sreg bya ba dang /sa'i sho ga gzhung bzhin
555-12(di): byas te/byin rlabs dang ldan par gyur pa de sa/ma mo'i sngon du bsnyen pa
555-13(di): gzung bar bya'o//sngon du bsnyen pa bgrangs nas rtags dang mtshan ma ma byung
555-14(di): ba des/bsgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor la 'jug par bya'o//bzang mo stobs kyi
555-15(di): srog gi rgyud las/gnas btsal zhing sngon du bsnyen pa'i le'u ste gnyis
555-16(di): de nas sgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor gru bzhi byas la/de ra ba'i rnam lnga dang
555-17(di): ra khrag dang /khyi khrag dang /phag khrag dang /khrag sna tshogs kyi chag chag
555-18(di): rnams gdab par bya'o//de nas shin tu 'thas par gyur pa dang /der khrag
555-19(di): gi thig le lnga byas la/rang rang gi srog snying po nas drangs te mchod pas mchod
556-1(di): par bya'o//de nas gnam thig gdab par bya'o//thig dang mkha' 'gro ma
556-2(di): gnyis su med par bstim la/rang bzhin gyi thig gdab par bya'o//de nas

556-4(di): 'grus thig btab pas re'u cha bzhi ru 'gyur ro//de la cha gsum du gcad de/
556-5(di): dbus kyi cha la da pa la chen po'i dkyil 'khor bya'o//gnyis pa la ni mkha' 'gro
556-6(di): ma rnams kyi dkyil 'khor bya'o//gsum pa la sgo dang brgyan bya'o//de bzhin
556-7(di): du tshon bkye bar bya'o//dkyil 'khor de'i dbus su gru gsum mthing nag go//
556-8(di): phyi rol 'khor lo rtsibs bzhi la e yis mtshan pa'o//sgo la dur khrod kyi rgyan gyis
556-9(di): brgyan pa'o//de dag gi mtha' ma na yang dur khrod kyi bya skad snyan par sgrog pa
556-10(di): bri bar bya'o//bzang mo stobs kyi rgyud las/dkyil 'khor bca' ba'i le'u ste
556-11(di): de ltar dkyil 'khor bzhengs nas/dkyil 'khor gyi yo byad srog par bya ste/
556-12(di): dpal chen po yab yum la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir/sha khrag sna tshogs pa'i gtor
556-13(di): ma dang /srid pa'i rgyal mo la sogs pa mchod pa'i phyir/sha snga brgya rtsa brgyad
556-14(di): dang khrag sna brgya rtsa brgyad dang /khyad par du yang snying chen po lnga yis byas
556-15(di): pa'i gtor ma bzhag par bya'o//thugs dam bskang ba'i rdzas su/baM snying
556-16(di): dang bya'i snying dang /khyi shel mig gi snying dang /lug kham rgya'i snying
556-17(di): rnams br+ha lcang lo can du dum bu bzhi pa'i nang du bdud rtsi rnam pa lnga dang
556-18(di): bsres la bzhag go//rten pa'i rten ni lug sgrag mos bya'o//bod pa'i rten ni
556-19(di): bard+ha'i snying khrag bard+ha gang la bya'o//zhing chen gyi g.yang gzhi dang /gzhan
557-1(di): yang rkang gi gling bu dang /sha chen gyi lang ling dang /'bar 'bur ne seg
557-2(di): dang /spyi blugs zhA/tshil chen gyi mar me kha bzhi dang /las kyi phur bu bzhi

557-9(di): 'bras bu'i du ga dang /khrag rnams sbyar ba'i nang du bar+ha dum bu drug pa'i nang
557-10(di): gang thun gyi rdzas su bzhag par bya'o//dusa dus su mkha' 'gro ma bod pa'i
557-11(di): g.yab mo/skyes pa lo nyi shu man chad lon pa'i lag pa g.yas pa'i mthil du srog
557-12(di): gi snying po bzhi bris la/'gugs pa'i rjes su bzhag par bya'o//de ltar rdzas
557-13(di): kyi sbyor ba tshogs pa dang ting nge 'dzin la 'jug par bya'o//bzang mo
557-14(di): stobs kyi rgyad las/dkyil 'khor gyi yo byad kyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o//

557-16(di): gzhal yas khang bsgom par bya'o//oM badzra shrI he ru ka ma hA kro d+hi shwa ri/zhes

557-18(di): dza ti pa la mi sha b+h+yo/zhes pa dang /saM trag rak+ha sha ma b+h+ya/zhes pa dang /
557-19(di): tra ga rak+ka ma badzra kI li ma b+h+ya/zhes bya ba ni/srid pa rgyal mo la sogs pa'i ma
557-20(di): mo bzhi po bskyed par bya'o//dza bzhis sbrugs la dbang byed pa bzhi bskyed par
558-1(di): bya'o//hUM bzhis rkan ma bzhi bskyed par bya'o//baM bzhis sgrol byed ma bzhi
558-2(di): bskyed par bya'o//de rnams kyi thugs kha nas bton te/de dag gi sngags ni
558-3(di): 'di dag go/tha ga rag+ha pa tra ru lu ru lu snying kha rag ma b+h+ya/trag rak+ka thag
558-4(di): rag+ha mu tra e ka dza ti/snying kha rag ma b+h+ya/ru ru ro ra tsa pa la snying khar mi sha
558-5(di): a shug me b+h+ya/tad+ya thA/ru ru ro ru be ti saM ba to saM b+h+yo/zhes srog gi snying

558-7(di): rag+ha sa ma ya snying ling rbud sa ma ya/thag rag+ha d gra bo mA ra ya snying ling rdud sa
558-8(di): ma ya/tra ga rag+ha dgra bo mA ra ya tsi+a snying ling rbud sa ma ya/zhes pas thugs
558-9(di): dam bskul bar bya'o//trag rag+ha snying kha rag snying khrag hur khu thun b+h+yo/trag
558-10(di): rag+ha snying khrag tshal pa thun b+h+ya/traga ra g+ha ma ma rag+ha thun b+h+yo/tha ga rag+ha snying rtsa
558-11(di): la zad thun b+h+yo/zhes thun d bab par bya'o//pa Hbu lu ru lu ma b+h+ya/b+h+ya raH
558-12(di): ma d+h+ya/ma ma rak+ha ma b+h+ya/ma ma ro sha ma b+h+ya/zhes phur bu la dgod par bya/

558-18(di): zhes dbang du bsdu bar bya'o//ma ma b+h+yo ru lu ru lu a ra li b+h+yo/zhes bya ba ni
558-19(di): bzhi snying gcig dril ba'i snying po 'dis las btsol bya'o//trag rag+ha sa ma ya
558-20(di): snying rtsa la thun b+h+yo/zhes bya ba ni bzhi snying gcig tu dril ba'i thun no//ma ma
558-21(di): thrag rag+ha baM ri l byi nana tsa pa la a ra li ru la b+h+yo/zhes bya ba ni bzhi snying gcig
559-1(di): tu dril ba'i bod pa'o//ru ru kye baM ti rak+ya d+ha la b+yo/zhes pa bzhi snying gcig
559-2(di): tu dril ba'i rbad pa'o//raga mo kaM kha rag kha thun snying la rag thun snying la rag

559-5(di): stobs kyi srog gi rgyud ces bya ba las/tina nge 'dzin dang sngags kyi le'u ste lnga

559-7(di): dbyings nas bzhengs//ma mo chen mo yal dam can//srid pa'i rgyal mo mthu
559-8(di): mo che//srid pa gsum la dbang b+byar zhing //sems can kun gyi snying 'byin

559-10(di): khrag dang zhag gi thig les brgyan//'gyur ba med pa rtags kyi dbu//srid
559-11(di): gsum bya ra mdzad pa'i spyan//khams gsum rngub par byed pa'i zhal//skye shi
559-12(di): rtsad nas gcod pa'i tshems//phyag ni rak+ta'i mtsho la rol//g.yas kyi phyag
559-13(di): drug phur bu ni/gsang ba mchog gi phur bu 'dril//'gro drug sems can
559-14(di): sgrol bar mdzad//g.yon gyi phyag drug phur bu ni//seng ldeng gdug pa'i phur

559-16(di): lo//srid pa'i ma mo mthu mo che//mig gcig gdang ni khro gnyer can//skra ni
559-17(di): nam mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam/sku ni mthing skrag chen mo la//da pral ba'i
559-18(di): dbyes na mig gcig ma//da bu la de ni ral gcig ma/bzhugs ni sa dgu'i
559-19(di): gting na bzhugs//chu mig kun gyi drod 'bebs ma//rlung nag 'tshub ma'i
560-1(di): sna 'dren ma//zhing sa kun gyi rkang yul ma//ched 'ga' rgya mtsho'i gting na
560-2(di): bzhugs//chu rnams kun gyi mdog 'byin ma/thams cad ma lus sdud byed

560-4(di): nyams ma//lo tog snar gyi bdag mo yin//de yi gsang ba'i mtshan gsol pa/

560-13(di): tsam gyis sa ma sleb//bdud kyi yum la gshin rje'i lcam//snang srid kun gyi byo

560-21(di): chug/naga mo chen mo mgyogs byed ma//bcol ba'i 'phrin las grub par
561-1(di): mdzod/khyed kyi 'khor na gnas pa ni//bran dang yang bran yang g.yog
561-2(di): bcas//sems can kun gyi bla tshe spud//las dang thun gyi bdag mo ste//

561-4(di): ba'i las rnams grub par mdzod//bzang mo stobs kyi rgyud las/bskul ba'i
561-5(di): de nas bcom ldan 'das ma sprul pa'i lha mo des 'di skad ces gsol to//
561-6(di): bcom ldan 'das cho ga 'di dag gi dbang bskur ba'i rim pa dang /drag po la
561-7(di): ji ltar bgyi bka' stsal du gsol/ces pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis

561-10(di): bskur ba ni srid pa'i rgyal mo la sogs pas bum pa'i dbang bskur te/khrag sna

561-15(di): ma ma ru ru a b+hi Shiny+tsa/ma ma ba ti ba ti sid+d+hi a b+hi Shiny+tsa/zhes dbang sbyin par
561-16(di): bya'o//dbang de dag bskur ba'i dus su yang /shun chen gyi g.yang gzhi la

561-18(di): cad kyi dbang shin tu thob bo//dbang de dag gi rdzas de dag thams cad kyang
561-19(di): spyod pa'i rdzas thams cad dang ldan par bya'o//bzang mo stobs kyi rgyud las/
562-1(di): de nas bsgrub thun bca' bar bya'o//dkyil 'khor mda' gang gru gsum du
562-2(di): brtsig par bya'o//de'i steng du gru gsum sor lnga da pangs su yod pa brtsig par
562-3(di): bya'o//de'i nang du dzo sol dang dur khrod kyi sol bas byug par bya'o//khrag sna
562-4(di): tshogs kyis chag chag gdab par bya'o//de zur gsum gyi phyogs gsum du gru

562-6(di): par bya'o//kha khyer thams cad la thod 'phreng btang bar bya'o//de'i phyi rim
562-7(di): la me ri btang ngo gru gsum re re'i da bus su seng ldeng gi phur bu dang
562-8(di): lcags kyi phur bu dang /mchog gi phur bu dang/dug shing las byas pa'i phur

562-11(di): pa khrag sna dgu dgu/ra du ga la sogs pa du ga sna da gu dang du ma bu drug pa
562-12(di): gang sbyar la bzhag go//shar phyogs su mi'i snying dang /seng ge'i snying dang

562-15(di): dpyid nya rul pa dang /khrag rul dang /snying du sbyar ba'i gtor ma dang
562-16(di): dusa da ba sbyar te mi yengs par sbyin par bya'o//de dag gi rgyu ba'i du sa ni/

562-19(di): g.yab pa'i rdzas dang ldan pas bsgrub par bya'o//yungs da kara dang/yungs

563-21(di): la ya/zhes bya'o//de ltar lha rnams gsal ba dang /dang por srog gi snying
564-1(di): por zhag d gur bgrang bar bya'o//de ltar grub pa'i rtags/rlung dang

564-20(di): ro//hUM zhes byas pas pad+ma rak+ta rngubs pas spyod pas dbabg du bsdu ba'o//che/
