584-1(di): chad dusa su//mkha' 'gro bdud du da bab pa'i phyir/ming gis med du mi
584-2(di): rung ngo //tshe dang skobs su de ltar bstan//de ru phyag rgya ji ltar dgod//
584-3(di): rgya shug tsan dan dkar po'i phur pa la//che mchog yab yum snying po bri//po
584-4(di): brang dbus su dgod par bya//mchog chen phur bu sor brgyad la//srid pa'i
584-5(di): rgyal mo'i snying po bri//shar gyi rtsibs la dgod par bya//seng ldeng phur pa
584-6(di): mtshan ldan la//e ka dza ti'i snying po bri//lho yi rtsibs la dgod par bya/
584-7(di): de nas lcags kyi phur bu la/nag mo re ma ti'i snying po bri//nub kyi rtsibs la
584-8(di): dgad par bya//de nas shes shing phur bu la//nag ma mgyogs byed tying ba
584-9(di): bri//byang gi rtsibs la dgod par bya//thugs rje rngam pa'i stobs ldan te/
584-10(di): rgyas chen dag gis brgyas la dgod//zabs kyi phur pa bzhi po la//'bar
584-11(di): ba'i snying po dza re bri//rang rang gi go gnas su dgod//bya phur bzhi yi
584-12(di): logs bzhi la//tshe 'phrog bzhi yi snying po b+h+yo re bri//rim pa bzhin du bkod
584-13(di): la bzhag skyer phur bzhi la gzer 'debs bzhi'i//snying po phaT re bris la
584-14(di): bzhag/thugs dam bskang rdzas su btsal ba yang //seng ge'i snying dang bam
584-15(di): gyi snying //de bzhin bya lug sgyug mo'i snying /sho na sgrag mo shal mig gi/
584-16(di): tsit+ta de bzhin ma nyams pa/kham rgya bya lug sgrug po'i snying tsiD+ta ma smad
584-17(di): bcas pa yang /bdud rtsi smyan dang sbyar ba yang /shar gyi zla kham dag la
584-18(di): dgod//ming gis med du mi rung ngo //dgod pa'i phur bu dag la yang //bri
584-19(di): ba'i snying por so sor gsungs//de nas mngon rdzogs rgyal po yis//mkha'
584-20(di): 'gro dbang du bsdu ba'i phyir//nyid kyi snying po 'di gsungs so//ma hA shI he
584-21(di): ta ka/ma hA kro d+hi shwa ri s+twaM/tsan dan dkar po'i phur pa la'o//yang /pa
585-1(di): la pa tra ru lu ru lu b+h+yo//de ni mchog gi phur pa la'o//e ka dza ti tsa pa la mi
585-2(di): sha b+h+yaM/de ni seng ldeng pur pa la'o//ra ti ru ti re ma ti b+h+ya/de ni lcags kyi
585-3(di): phur pa la'o//ma ma b+h+ya ro sha ma b+h+yao/de ni snying gi phur pa la'o//snying po de
585-4(di): rnams so sor yang /gro gshing shun dag la bri//phur pa re re'i mgul du
585-5(di): gdags//zhes gsungs so//tsit+ta snying lung chen po las/tshe bskabs
585-6(di): bstan pa dan phur pa bri zhing dkyil 'khor du dgod pa bstan pa'i le'u ste gsum
585-7(di): de nas yang ni bshad par bya//dpyid zla gsum la bsgrub pa'i tshe/
585-8(di): mkha' lding nag mo'i snying dang mig/de dang stag gi snying la sogs//snying snga
585-9(di): lnga dang sbyar ba'i gtor ma bya//dbyar gyi tshe na me tog sna tshogs dang snying
585-10(di): sna tshogs sbyar ba'o//dgun ni shing chen phur mo sregs pa'i thal ba//rkang
585-11(di): chen bsregs pa'i thal ba dang //gong gi snying sna lnga dang sbyar//dusa bzhi'i
585-12(di): gtor ma de ltar btang /las rnams thams cad 'grub par bshad//de
585-13(di): nas 'brub khyang bshad bya ba//rgya cu khru gang zabs su khru phyed do//bang rim
585-14(di): gsum dang ldan par bya//nang du phung sri'i thod pa la//snying po raM gyis bskor
585-15(di): la dgod//kha ru ling ga ma nyams gzhug/ling ga rdzas kyi mtshan nyid ni/
585-16(di): dgra bo'i rjes sam khang pa yang //dgra bo'i yul phyogs kyi 'dzag ma'am/
585-17(di): gnam gyi dum bu de rnams la//dgra bo'i gzugs byas kha ru gzhug/ces
585-18(di): gsungs so//tsit+ta snying lung chen mo las/dus bzhi'i gtora ma dang sbyar ba
585-19(di): dang /mA ra ya gsad pa'i dkyil 'khor bstan pa'i le'u bshad pa'o//
586-1(di): de nasa yum chen mo'i mkha' nas/lcags kyi sdig pa mgo dgu pa cig byung
586-2(di): nas/ngan sngags gsad pa 'di gsungs so/dbus kyi gtso bo bskyed pa'i
586-3(di): sngags ni/thag rak+ha pa tra b+ya/rtag tu srog nas drang ba'i snying po ni/thag
586-4(di): rak+ka pa tra ru lu ru lu snying kha ra ga ma b+h+yo/las la bskul ba'i tshe na yang /snying
586-5(di): po sngags 'disa zad par bya'o//trag rak+ka snying kha rag ma b+h+yo/nr-i dza dgra bo mA
586-6(di): ra ya phaT/ye ka dza TaM bskyed pa'i snying po ni/b+h+yoa raHa ma b+h+ya/srog nas drang
586-7(di): ba'i snyang po ni/tha ba raH/thag raH/ta tha ye ka dza TaM/snying kha rag ma
586-8(di): b+h+yo/rbad pa'i snying po ni/trag rak+tha sa ma ya/dgra bo'i tsit+ta snying ling rbud/
586-9(di): re ma ti bskyed pa'i snying po ni/ma ma raka dz+ya/srog nas drang ba'i snying po ni/
586-10(di): ru ru ro ru tsa pa la a shug me b+h+yo/rbad pa'i snying po ni/tra ga rak+a dgra bo'i
586-11(di): tsit+ta snying ling zad/mgyogs byed ma bskyed pa'i snying po ni/ma ma rak+ta badzra
586-12(di): ga la b+ya/srog nas drang ba'i snying po ni/yaM ta d+ya thA ru ru tra ru be ti sam po ta
586-13(di): si swA hA/zbad pa'i snying po ni/thag raHa dgra bo mA ra ya snying bhud/de nas
586-14(di): bsam pa'i bye brag bstan pa ni//bdag nyid che mchog he ru kar bsgoms te/
586-15(di): de yi thugs khar ye shes sems dpa'o//snying po sngags kyis bskor bar bsam/
586-16(di): tra ga rak+kha pa tra ru lu ru lu snying kha raga ma b+h+yo/zhes bzlas pas/las 'di
586-17(di): 'phros pas bde bar gshegs pa'i thugs dam gyi rgyud bsgral/'gro ba sems
586-18(di): can gyi don byas/rig 'dzin rnams kyi bsam pa bskyed/ma mo rnams kyi
586-19(di): 'dod pa gnas su bskang /slara saM ha ra na hUM zhes 'dus/bdag gi lus
586-20(di): ngag yid gsum gyi bag chags kyi skyon gyi sgrib pa ni sbyangs/de nas 'od 'phros
587-1(di): pas/'od dgra bo la phog pas lus ngag yid gsum rdul phran bzhin brlag/
587-2(di): phar 'phro tshur 'phro mgal me klad la bskor ba bzhin bya'o//tsit+ta snying lung chen
587-3(di): po las/srog gi skabs su drang ba'i snying po bstan pa dang bsam pa'i bye brag
587-4(di): 'du cu zod gtong ji ltar lags//'di yi rbod gtong mtshan nyid ni//
587-5(di): 'bar ba bzhi yis srog thugs 'phrog/tshe 'phrog bzhi yis lus srog phrol/
587-6(di): gzer 'debs bzhi yis gnas gzhir gzer//'du ru da bab dusa ji ltar
587-7(di): lags//shwa dang bu yug ser ba dang //rlung dang tshub nag byung na grub/
587-8(di): gdab pa'i drag sngags ji ltar lags//gzer 'debs bzhi yi drag sngags ni//
587-9(di): iny+dza zhes pa spyi gtsug tu//d kar mo dung gi thor tshugs can//khyan gi mgo la
587-10(di): nyi zla'i spyan can phur pas gdaba/pra pa dza zhes pa dpral bar gdaba/
587-11(di): 'jigs byed chen mo dpal gyi yum/ral pa kham nag gyen du 'khyil//ces
587-12(di): so//pra li dza zhes pas snying khar gdaba/sngon mo lcags kyi thor tshugs can//
587-13(di): khyung gi mgo la nyi zla'i spyan gyis so//sring gi dza zhes pas/lte ba'i dkyil du
587-14(di): phur bus gda ba//sngon mo mi bsrun dpal gyi yum gyis so//zhes phur pas
587-15(di): gdab bo//de nas phur pa la yang srid pa'i rgyal mor bsgoms la/pa tra yang
587-16(di): song song zhes yid la bzlas pas/de na sa mA ra yak+ta byas pas/khrag tu gyur
587-17(di): par bsam mo//de nas raM raM byas pas/phur pa slar la gdab bo//pra shaM haM
587-18(di): brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas pas/da ci br+d+ha rak+tas bkang ba de'i thun phyung la 'phang
587-19(di): ngo //de nas ling ga mnan pa bya//zhes pa ni/thod pa dum bu drug pa la
588-1(di): 'khor lo rtsibs bcu gnyis bya/rtsibs mtshan bcu gnyis snying po ming gis
588-2(di): da gang dza zhes pa yi snying pos mnan//ba su ta yi thag pas bcing //de
588-3(di): yi dbus su ling ga gzhug/dur khrod chen po yi dbus su mnan//zhes
588-4(di): gsungs so//tsit+ta snying lung chen po las/bod gtong gi mtshan nyid bstan pa
588-5(di): dang /gdab pa'i dmigs bstan pa dang /mnan pa'i las bstan pa'i le'u
588-6(di): de nas thun gyi dkyil 'khor bstan pa ni/dang po dkyil 'khor 'dom phyed
588-7(di): gru gsum chag gang bar brtsig go//de'i steng du gru gsum de bas mtho bar
588-8(di): brtsig go//de'i nang du thun khung bzhir dzo sol dang ro bsregs pa'i thal bas
588-9(di): bkang la nag por bya'o//de'i steng du kha khyer la thod 'phreng gis bskor ro//
588-10(di): de'i phyi rim la me ri btang /gsum gyi kha khyer la/de'i dbus su phur pa cig
588-11(di): la srid pa'i rgyal mor mnga' gsol dgod/phur pa gsum la e ka dza ti la
588-12(di): sogs pa/phur pa gsum la mnga' gsol la thun khung gru gsum du bzhugs su
588-13(di): gsol lo//gangs kyi dum bu la sogs pa la byas pa'i ling ga bzhi yang thun khyung
588-14(di): bzhir bzhag go//phyar pa rnams la rgyas chen gyi gzungs zag gis gtso phur
588-15(di): dang spel/mon la sogs pa'i rabs chad pa'i thod pa bzhi thun khung bzhir
588-16(di): gzhug/dbus su bsag pa'i dam rdzas bstan pa ni/bya lug sgyug mo'i snying
588-17(di): dang khyi sgrag po shel mig dang /lug kham rgya sgrug mo'i snying dang /khrag snga
588-18(di): tshogs bsres pas ban d+ha dgang ngo /dkyil 'khor gyi steng du bzhag go//
588-19(di): thun snod gsum du med du mi rung ba'i rdzas bstan pa ni/mang la khrag/
589-1(di): mo'i khrag rims khrag/bsngol 'khrig mo'i khrag/yug sa mo'i khrag/ra khyi
589-2(di): phag gi khrag/sbyang khyi'i khrag/du ga sna dgu/de rnams thun la dgos so//
589-3(di): snying sna de med du mi rung ngo //khrag sna de rnams med du med rung ngo
589-4(di): lug sgug mo la 'ben bya'o//mi snying khrag la bod do//de nas che mchog he ru
589-5(di): ka gtso gzung sa dang bcas pa/'khor srid pa'i rgyal po la sogs pa gzer
589-6(di): 'de basa ma yan chad bskyed la/dkyil 'khor chen pos mnyes par bya/
589-7(di): grangs kyi srog snying zhag dgu drang //mtshan ma rtags rnams byung ba dang
589-8(di): thun gyi las la sbyar bar bya//zhes gsungs so//tsit+ta snying lung chen po las/
589-9(di): thun gyi dkyil 'khor bstan pa dang /de ru dgod pa'i dam rdzas bstan pa dang
589-10(di): grangs kyi bsnyen pa bya ba'i thabs bstan pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o//
589-11(di): de nas thun brab pa'i rdzas bstan pa ni/srid pa'i rgyal mo'i thun la yungs
589-12(di): ka ra la bsngags la khrag la bstim/dza ti ma'i yungs na ga la bzlas la khrag la
589-13(di): bstim/nag mo re ma ti skye tshe la bzlas shing khrag la bstim/nag mo mgyogs
589-14(di): byed ma gu gul la bzlas la khrag la bstim/thun sgrub pa'i dusa ni khol lam/
589-15(di): rlabs pa byang na grub rtags yin no//de nas las la sbyar bar byas te/
589-16(di): bdag nyid srid pa'i rgyal mor sku bskyed do/de'i thugs kha nasa zhe sdang rdzo rje'i
589-17(di): skur bsgyur la ra rog po la zhon/gdong pa khrag gis bsgyur/skra spyi bor
589-18(di): bcings/lus snen mor bya/shun gyi g.yang gzhi bgo/kha thur du blta/'o
589-19(di): dod bod/dpang gzugs/tshe nges gda ba/gza' gdug pa la rtsis la
589-20(di): gdaba/ces gsungs so//tsi+a snying lung chen po'i rgyud las/thun la brab
590-1(di): ba'i rdzas bstan pa dang /thun 'phang ba'i dgongs pa bstan pa'i le'u ste brgyad
590-2(di): de nas yang dag bshad par bya/thun gyi dmad mo'i 'khor lo dang /
590-3(di): 'gugs la sogs pho nya'i srog snying so sor bstan/pur bu dag la bri ba'i snying
590-4(di): po ni/yaM pa tra ru lu ru lu b+h+yo/nyi ma shar kar bzlas pa ni/rtag tu srog
590-5(di): nas drang ba'i snying po ste 'di'o//thrag rag+ha pa tra ru lu ru lu snying kha rag ma b+h+yo/
590-6(di): gzong gis brgyab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/thun gyi sngags te trag rak+kha mu tra ma ma
590-7(di): thun b+h+yo/khra rgod gzan la 'dzum pa ltar bzlas pa ni/bskul tshig la gdags
590-8(di): pa'i sngags te/thrag rakya snying kha rag ma b+h+yaM/nri dza d gra bo mA ra ya phaT/
590-9(di): gser gyi thog gi brgyab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/ma mo skya bskyed pa'i sngags te/
590-10(di): trag rakya dgra bo pa tra ma ma b+h+yo/rin po che bstar la brgyas pa ltar bzlas pa ni/
590-11(di): bri ba'i sngags te/e ka dza ti tsa pa la mi sha b+h+yo/nyi ma shar ba ltar bzlas pa ni/
590-12(di): srog sngags te/trag rak+Sa tha g rak+a mu tre e ka dza ti snying kha rag ma b+h+yaM/gser
590-13(di): gyi thog brgyab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/skra bskyed pa'i sngags te/b+h+ya rak+Sha ma b+h+yo/
590-14(di): gzong gis brgyab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/thun la gdags pa'i sngags te/tra ga
590-15(di): rak+a ma snying khrag tshal pa thun b+h+ya/khra rgod gzan la 'jug pa ltar bzlas pa ni/
590-16(di): bskul tshig la gdags pa'i sngags te/tra ga rak+tha sa ma ya dgra bo'i tsit+ta snying
590-17(di): ling rbud/gser gyi thog gis brgyab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/skra bskyed pa'i
590-18(di): sngags te/ma ma rak+a ma b+h+ya/rin po che gcar gyi tshul du bzlas pa ni/bri
590-19(di): ba'i sngags te/ra ti ru ti re ma ti/nyi ma shar ltar bzlas pa ni/skyed pa
591-1(di): rkyen gyi sngags te/ru ru ro ru tsa pa la mi sha ya shug me/seng ge tshang ma sha la
591-2(di): ru ba pa ltar bzlas pa ni/bskul tshig gi mjug tu gdags pa'i sngags te/
591-3(di): thrag rag+ha dgra bo'i tsit+ta snying ling bad/mtshon cha'i char d bab pa ltar bzlas pa
591-4(di): ni/thun la gdags pa'i sngags te/tra ga rag+ha snying khrag hu bu thun b+h+yo/
591-5(di): 'khor los brgyab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/thun gyi dus su sku bskyed pa'i sngags te/
591-6(di): ma ma rak+ha badzra ka ra lam b+h+ya/lcags kyi tsha tsha 'phro ba ltar bzlas pa ni/phur
591-7(di): pa la bri'o//mtshon cha sna tshogs pa'i 'khor las/mtshon cha sna tshogs byung ba
591-8(di): lnar bzas pa ni/thun gyi srag nas drang ba'o//ta d+ya thA/tsa ru ra tsa be ti sa
591-9(di): be to si swA hA/seng ge tshang ma sha la rub pa ltar bzlas pa ni/thrag rak+Sha dgra
591-10(di): bo mA ra ya snying ling bhud/tshon sna lnga'i char dbab pa ltar bzlas pa ni/thun
591-11(di): la gdags pa'i snying pos te/tra ga rak+a ma snying rtsa khrag ti bad bu thun b+h+ya/
591-12(di): 'gyed pa dang mdud pa gsum gyi tshul du bzlas pa ni/po nya gsum gyi srog snying
591-13(di): kha rag ma b+h+ya/bla rkun ma bzhi'o//snying kha rag nri dza dgra bo mA ra ya phaT/
591-14(di): tshe 'phrog ma bzhi'o//rag+lo taM ki dza dza kha le ma thun b+h+yo/gzer 'debs ma
591-15(di): bzhi'o//thugs rje nyi ma'i rgyan ltar bzlas pa dang /srin po ro la rngam stabs
591-16(di): kyi bzlas pa ni/tshe'i snying po'i dusa dana dbang du bsdu ba'i dus so//snying
591-17(di): khrag rtsal breng breng /oM dz+wa la dz+wa la hUM/ru ru ra ru tsa pa la A ya She ya/tsit+ta
591-18(di): snying lung chen po'i rgyud las/thun gyi snying po bstan pa dang /bla 'gugs la
591-19(di): sogs pa'i snying po dang /tshe dbang du bsdu ba'i snying po bstan pa'i le'u ste dgu
592-1(di): de nas d bab pa'i dus tshod ji ltar lags/thun khrag khol ba'i dus so//
592-2(di): ling ga chen por gd ba sngags ji ltar lags/i na dza klad pa'i rgyas su ste/
592-3(di): ni dza lhag pa'i bdud du'o//dpral ni mig la ste//pra pe dza ni sna la ste/
592-4(di): saM re ni dza ni lce la'o//mA ra yag+ha mgrin pa ste//gsang ba'i mchog phur bzhi
592-5(di): po yis//rim pa bzhin du gdab par gsungs//raM raM dza ni dpung mgo ste/
592-6(di): yaM rag+ha dza ni snying la'o//rmig rag+ha dza ni po la'o//kaM ye rag+ha dza ni lte ba'o//
592-7(di): tsit+ta rag+ha dza ni snying kha la'o//pra sha haM ni rkang mthil du gdab//kI la ya dang
592-8(di): sbyar bar gsungs//g.yon gyi seng phur pa la'o//bka' 'di gang la bstan
592-9(di): pa'i gnas//theg pa che la mos pa dang /lus dang srog la mi lta zhing //bu
592-10(di): dang chung ma rgyan gyis brgyan/rtag tu zlos la brtson pa dang /ngan sngags
592-11(di): sbyar ba rgyan mi gtsod /sha khrag longs spyod byed la bstan//gzhan la bstan
592-12(di): pa ra ma gsungs so//tsit+ta snying lung chen po'i rgyud las/gdab pa'i dusa tshod
592-13(di): daba gdaba pa'i sngags dang /bshad pa'i gang zag la sogs pa las kyi
592-14(di): rim pa'i rgyud chen po yum srid pa'i zhal nas gsungs pa/tsit+ta snying lung gi
592-15(di): bi ma la mi tras sbang gi btsas su bzhag pa rdzogs so//
593-1(di): rgya gar skad du/mu phya ma thag tsakra tar+ha/bod skad du/ye
593-2(di): nyid phyi mo mthar thug gi rgyud/gsang sngags mthar thug 'khor lo ste//gtan
593-3(di): la dbab pa'i don bshad na//gnas pa rtsa/g.yo ba rlung /bkod pa yi ge/
593-4(di): rtsa ba byang chub sems/gnyug ma ni mi 'gyur ba'i g.yung drung mi shigs
593-5(di): pa'i dzo rje/mi nub pa'i rgyal mtshan/thams cad 'byung ba'i yul de'o//
593-6(di): dbyibs dang kha dog 'di ltar snang //byang sems gnyug ma'i 'od zer dang /
593-7(di): 'gyur med rtag chen g.yung drung las//'phrin las rnam bzhi rdzogs 'char bas/
593-8(di): zhi ba'i skru grub dkar la zlum//rgyas pa'i sku grub gru bzhi ser//dbang gi skya
593-9(di): grub zla kham dmar//mngon spyod skya grub rnam shes brgyad//'khor lo brgyad
593-10(di): kyi gzhal yas dang //sna tshogs sprul pa'i tshogs brgyad nas//'gro ba'i don
593-11(di): du yang dag 'phros//'phrin las skyur grub gru gsum gnag/rnam shes brgyad
594-1(di): kyi yul brgyad do//ye phyi 'khor lo zhes pa ni//'gyur ba med pas ye nyid la/
594-2(di): thams cad 'byung bas phyi ma'o//'khor ba'i gdar bcad sems la ltos/
594-3(di): man ngag gtan phebs 'khor lo'o//ye phyi thugs kyi 'khor lo las//dam
594-4(di): ches gsang sngags rtsa srog tu//yig smad drug cu rtsa gnyis gnas//yi ge phyi yi
594-5(di): mtshan nyid ni//thugs rje 'od 'phros 'di ltar snang /tha ma bzhi bcu rtsa
594-6(di): gnyis ni/rin chen nor bu brgyad pa 'dra//yan lag bcu drug mtshan nyid
594-7(di): ni//gu gu thugs rje lcags kyu 'dra//zhabs skyed skom chen skyil grang 'dra/
594-8(di): 'greng bu rtsal chen dbyug tho 'dra//na ro thang dkar lding ba 'dra//ya ta gyad
594-9(di): pa'i sgras btsugs 'dra//phra gnyis skar chen shar ba 'dra//gtsod bzhi sbrul
594-10(di): nag 'khyud pa 'dra//a ni yab yum gnyis med don sdud pa'o//gsang don
594-11(di): nges pa'i a lnga ni//'gyur med g.yung drung chos ska la/skye ba'i cho 'phrul
594-12(di): rtags bstan pa'o//a ti rtse mo byung rgyal de/ma bcos gnyug ma'i don
594-13(di): bstan phyir//a gcig dbyings shad lta bu 'dra//byang sems thig le gnyug
594-14(di): ma'i bang /'gyur med g.yung drung shar na gnas//yab yum thabs dang
594-15(di): shes raba las//dbyings danga ye shes don bstan phyir//i gcig brtsos pa'i
594-16(di): ra dang 'dra//byang sems thig le gnyug ma yi//'gyur med g.yung drung lto na
594-17(di): gnas//snang srid gtan la da bab pa dang//sems can thugs rjes bzung
594-18(di): ba'i phyir//a gcig ba dan phyar ba 'dra//byang sems thig le gnyug ma yi/
594-19(di): 'gyur med g.yung drung nuba na gnas//gzugs phung lta ru bsgrub pa dang /
594-20(di): nga bdag mi gzung mnyam bzhag phyir//ya gcig seng ge kha dbyal 'dra/
594-21(di): byang sems thig le gnyug ma yi//'gyur med g.yung drung byabg na gnas/
595-1(di): bas mi 'dul dus byang na/drag po'i 'phrin las mthar dbyung phyir//a
595-2(di): gcig lha tshangs mig dang 'dra//byang sems thig le gnyug ma yi//'gyur
595-3(di): med g.yung drung dbus na gnas//thams cad ji ltar gnas shes na//a lnga
595-4(di): byang sems gzhug ma yi//rtag chen g.yung drung sku la gnas//ka lnga rnam shes
595-5(di): shar gling gi//zla kham kang ka ri la gnas//ta lnga rnam shes lto gling gi/
595-6(di): sogs ka bai DUR+Ya la gnas//pa lnga rnam shes nu ba gling gi//zum po ra ga dmar
595-7(di): la gnas//rtsa lnga rnam shes byang gling gi//gru bzhi ir+t+ha ldang la gnas//tsa lnga
595-8(di): rnam shes shar lho'i gling //dzo rdze rgya gram gser la gnas//Ta lnga rnam shes lto
595-9(di): nub gling //rin chen rgya gram skrag la gnas//zha lnga rnam shes byang chub gling /
595-10(di): pad+ma rgya gram dkar la gnas//ra lnga rnam shes byang shar gling //'khor lo rgya
595-11(di): gram lcags la gnas//yang daga nges pa'i don du na//gsang ba'i yi ge a lnga
595-12(di): ni//sems nyid yi ge gsang gter gyi//'gyur med g.yung drung sku la gnas/
595-13(di): ka smad bzhi bcu tham pa ni//nam shes tshogs brgyad gzhal yas khang
595-14(di): gser gyi 'khor lo rtsibs brgyad dkyil//sna tshogs sprul pa'i mkha' na gnas/
595-15(di): rnam shes brgyad kyi yul brgyad na//yan lag yi ge bcu drug gnas//de yi
595-16(di): 'phrin las mthar phyin yi ge'o//rgya rkyen don sbyor mthar sdud pa'i//yan
595-17(di): lag yi ge bcu drug pa'o//rims sam ji ltar gnas she na//shar nas rim
595-18(di): med 'khor bar gnas//ye nyid phyi mo'i 'khor lo yi//'od zer 'phro 'du de
595-19(di): ltar gnas//de don gtan la dbab pa'i phyir//bla ma brgyad pa'i man ngag
595-20(di): dang //rtog pa rnam bzhi gtan la dbab//bla ma brgyad pa'i man ngag ni//
595-21(di): gsang sde bco brgyad 'gyon la thug/rtog pa rnam bzhi zhes pa ni//rang
596-1(di): bzhin rnam par tshog pa dang /zhar las byung ba'i rtog pa dang /ched du
596-2(di): byas pa'i rtog pa dang //nga bdag 'dzin pa'i rtog pa'o//rnam rtog bzhi yis
596-3(di): spyad brtags la//ka la sogs te bzhi bcu gnyis//gnas brgyad dag nas
596-4(di): drang bar bya//snying kha mgrin pa rkan dana lce/so dang mchu sna spyi bo gtsug
596-5(di): zhi rgyas dbang dang mngon spyod 'phrin las lnga//sbha yi bye brag shes par bya'o//
596-6(di): mthar thug sgra yi bye brag ni//shes pa rnam bzhis gtan la da bab//ma rig
596-7(di): 'khrul pas shes pa dang /rtog spyod lam gyis shes pa dang rigs ldan
596-8(di): mkhas pa'i shes pa dang //ma btsos gnyug ma'i shes pa'o//shes pa rnam bzhi
596-9(di): mdu da bkrol la//de ba bsad kyi sgra bcu yi//'gyur med g.yung drung thig le
596-10(di): dang gsang ba thugs kyi gnas bcu nas//yig smad drug cu rtsa gnyis
596-11(di): grangs//ga bcu'i don du gang zhe na//ya mu ta dabg b+ha ling ta//dza du ta
596-12(di): dang d+ha ri ta/ka ta taM dang b+hi ru ta/dza 'u ta dang 'u rga ta//sha li ta dang
596-13(di): 'du 'phro ta'o//a lnga byang sems gnyug ma'o//'gyur med g.yung drung thig
596-14(di): le nas//ya mu ta yi spra grangs la//gsang don mthar thug thams cad gro//
596-15(di): ka lnga shar gyi 'khor lo dang //kang kha zla kham mkhar nang nas//g+ha li ta yi sgra
596-16(di): grangs la//snying srog rtsa nas thams cad spo//tsa lnga lho shar 'khor lo dang
596-17(di): gser gyi dzo rje'i mkhar nana nas//dza du ta yi sgra grangs la//mgrin pa'i
596-18(di): gsang nas thams cad spho//ta lnga lho yi 'khor lo dang //bai DA ra sogs kha'i
596-19(di): mkhar nang nas//da ri ta yi sgra grangs la//rkan gyi bar nas thams cad sgo//
596-20(di): Ta lnga lho nub 'khor lo dang //rin chen stug po'i mkhar nang nas//Da ra ta
596-21(di): yei sgra grangs la//lce yi dkyil nas thams cad +ho//pa lnga nub kyi 'khor lo

597-2(di): rtse nas thams cad sgo/rtsa lnga byang gi 'khor lo dang //ir+d+ha gru bzhi'i mkhar

597-4(di): lnga byang chub 'khor lo dang //pad+ma dkar po'i mkhar nang nas//'u rga ta yi
597-5(di): sgra grangs la//sna yi sbrubs nas thams cad spo//ra lnga byang shar 'khor lo
597-6(di): dang //lcags ka 'khor lo mkhar nang nas//sha li ta yi spra grangs la//spyi
597-7(di): bo'i gtsug nas thams cad to//gu gu la sogs yan lag rnams//'du 'phro
597-8(di): ta yi sgra grangs la//rnam shes brgyad kyi yul brgyad na//rim min 'khor bar
597-9(di): gnas pa ni//skabs sbyar dgos 'brel don dam du//man ngag can gyis
597-10(di): gtan la dbab//yang na man ngag mchog la thug/gsang ba mthar thug man
597-11(di): ngag tu//gsang sngags sgra la mang na yang //sgra yi rtsa ba bzhir 'dus te/
597-12(di): gyen mthur du rgyug pa'i sgra dang /mel bzhi ru 'gro ba'i sgra dang /'bros
597-13(di): 'ded kyi sbra dang /rang zad du gnas pa'i sgra'o//gyen thur du gyug pa'i sbra la
597-14(di): wa/gyen du ghyug pa'i thra gsum/thur du rgyug pa'i shra gnyis so//gyen du
597-15(di): gyug pa'i sgra gsum ni//lcags kya rgod lding dbyug zo'o//thur du rgyug pa'i sgra

597-18(di): 'bros pa'i sgra la dgu/'ded pa'i sgra la lnga'o//'bros pa'i sba dgu ni/
597-19(di): 'ded pa'i skra lnga ni/gda'/dka'/bka'/mkha'/'ga'/rang
597-20(di): zad du gnas pa'i pra ni/yan lag gis kyang ma 'brel ba'o//gdab sar
598-1(di): shar gnad sa/don dam dgos 'brel snga rnams ni//rang bzhin 'od zer
598-2(di): shugs kyis 'byung ngo /gsang ba mthar thug man nag tu//gsang sngags sgra

598-5(di): rang skad sgra yis grangs pa dang //bdud kyi rang skad sgra yis grangs pa dang/
598-6(di): srin po'i rang skad sgra yis grangs pa dang //lha mo'i rang skad sgra yis grangs pa
598-7(di): dang gnod sbyin rang skad sgra yis grangs pa dang //mu stegs rang skad dgra
598-8(di): yis grangs pa dang brgyad da//de la gsang sngags sgra yis gsang dan du/a
598-9(di): de daga la bzhi bzhir grangs/rang skad re la sgra bzhi yis bod pa'o//sad
598-10(di): kara tsa las grangs pa ni//lha yi rgyal po bod pa'i sgra'o//de ba ge tra yas
598-11(di): grangs pa//lta bdud dregs chen bod pa'i sgra'o//sod de ba ra ma yas grangs
598-12(di): pa//lha bran po mo bod pa'i sgra'o//kha la ma 'dusa yas grangs pa//yul lha
598-13(di): yul sman bod pa'i sgra//nA ga rA dza yas grangs pa//klu yi rgyal po bod pa'i
598-14(di): sbra'o//nA ga de ba yas grangs pa//kru yi sgra rnams bod pa'i sgra'o//nA ga ma
598-15(di): ge yas grangs pa//gru bdud po mo bod pa'i sgra'o//nA ga ya ma yas grangs pa/
598-16(di): ha la ti mgo dgu bod pa'i sgra'o//ya ma ra dza yas grangs pa/gshin rje yab
598-17(di): yum bod pa'i sgra'o//ya ma tri yas drabs pa ni//gshin rdze gsang byed bod
598-18(di): pa'i sgra'o//ya ma ge raga yas grangs pa ni//gshin rjze pho bdud mo bdud bod
598-19(di): pa'i sgra'o//ya ma tri ta yas grabgs pa ni//gshin rje phung byed bod pa'i sgra'o//
598-20(di): ge ya na ga shi yas grangs pa//naga mo bkrag med bod pa'i sgra'o//ge srib ni
598-21(di): su pa yas grangs pa ni//gsang ba mi rkun bod pa'i sgra'o//ge ti ba ta ra sha
599-1(di): yas grangs pa//shan pa phung byed bod pa'i sgra'o//ge ba pu ga ti yas grangs
599-2(di): pa dang /lkug pa 'dzu myul bod pa'i sgra'o//dkrags shig pus ta ra yas
599-3(di): grangs pa//'da' ka 'gri ba bod pa'i sgra'o//thrag sha tsa ta yas grangs la//
599-4(di): lang ka mgrin bcu bod pa'i sgra'o//rag sha bu yis yas grangs pa//chag gar
599-5(di): lag rings bod pa'i sgra'o//khrag sha du ma ta yas drabs pa//srin po khrag mig
599-6(di): bod pa'i sgra'o//ma ma ru lus yas grangs pa//dbyings kyi lha mo bod pa'i

599-9(di): grangs pa//las kyi ma mo bod pa'i sgra'o//hri tsa ag shu yas grangs pa/

599-17(di): ngag tu//bod sgra sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis//chos skyong srung ma dam can rnams/
599-18(di): gtan la phab na sa las la 'bod//bka' la btags nas las la 'gyed/

600-1(di): thog ser nad kyi sbyin thang 'bebs//ltas ngan cho 'phrul sna tshogs gtong

600-18(di): gsang sngags zhi khro rab 'byams la//rgyas pa 'dus pa mdo dang mthar thug
600-19(di): bzhi//phyi slob nang slob sems nyid don bsdus la//de gsang ba rin po che'i
600-20(di): 'khor lo'o//badzra ti ba ta tsakra'i sbras//ya man ta ka phur pa'i lha//gsang

601-1(di): ti ru rag tsakra'i sgras//lha mo yag sha dam can ma'i//gsang ba 'khrul 'khor
601-2(di): gtan la 'bebs//de btags pa shes ra ba yig 'bru'i 'khor lo'o//zla mig

601-4(di): 'khor gtan la 'bebs//de nyid ma bzhi pa'i 'khor lo'o//sa 'du a kri

602-1(di): dog gzugs su 'dod//ji ltar sna tshogs gang yang yin//ye phyi gnyug ma'i

603-1(di): stegs ngan sngags kyi//mdo rtsa yang snying mthar thug rnams/sde brgyad rang

603-11(di): 'bru dgu dang //ma mo'i yang snying ru lu 'bru dgu la//gling bzhi skad sgra yis

603-13(di): blangs la//yan lag yi ge tshod lding rgyan/shar phyogs gling gi byang dkyil

603-15(di): srog yi ge ma blangs la//steng nas gsang ba'i thig les mdzes//byang nub
603-16(di): gling gi lta gling nas//lha srog yi ge 'a blangs la//gru gsum cho 'phrul ya las
603-17(di): 'brel//gsang ba 'dus pa'i snying po de//bkra shis dbang ldan phyir na gling
603-18(di): du bzhag/nub phyogs gling gi lho gling nas//lha srog yi ge ba blangs la/
603-19(di): byang phyogs gling gi gling dkyil nas//lha srog yi ge dza dang ldan//byang shar
603-20(di): gling gi gling dkyil nas//lha srog yi ge ra yis spel//de 'og drag po phra yis
603-21(di): bcad//shar phyogs gling gi gling dkyil nas//lha srog yi ge ka blangs la//
604-1(di): yan lag yi ge lcags kyis brgyan//byang shar gling gi shar gling nas//lta srog
604-2(di): yi ge la blangs la//yan lag yi ge lcags kyus brgyan//de 'og drag po phra
604-3(di): yig bcad//shar phyogs gling gi gling dkyil nas//lha srog yi ge ka blangs la//

604-5(di): yi ge la blangs la//byang nub gling gi byang dkyil nas/lta srog yi ge ya
604-6(di): blangs la//rang rang phra yis bcad la bzhag/gling brgyad dbus kyi byang gling
604-7(di): nas//lha srog yi ge ha blangs la//byang nuba gling gi lho gling nas//lha srog

604-10(di): la//yan lag yi ge skyil krung brgyan//lho phyogs gling gi byang gling nas//lha

626-13(di): bram ze gdol pa nyi shu lnga//dmangs rigs zhes su gsungs pa yin/bya rog

626-15(di): gdol pa 'dzin shes gtong mi shes//de yi lo yang gdol pa ste//za ba
626-16(di): yang ni gdol pa yin//zhag kyang gdol pa yin pas na//dus tshod kyang ni
626-17(di): gdol pa'o//phyogs dang 'byung brtags dang ni//gdol pa nyi shu rtsa lnga yin/
627-1(di): nag po gdal pa'i khram gyi skabs te nyer drug pa'o/
627-2(di): sprul pa rigs lnga'i kyu bdag 'di//gshin dze ya ma rA dza shar/lan
627-3(di): myos byed 'bar ba gnas//nub na yatsa ma cu'o//byang na ral pa tshar dgu
627-4(di): yin//byang shar 'chi bdag nag po yin//kru yi rigs kyi bdag po ste/
627-5(di): gshin rje zhes sam 'chi ba'i bdag/gnam khram thams cad de yi bye//gshin
627-6(di): de nas gsang ba'i ye shes chen po shar ba ni/mo be la huM/skra'i 'bru
627-7(di): bzhi pa'/ha ka/zaM tsa/thugs kyi 'bru bzhi pa'o//ye ba khram cu
627-8(di): gsung gi 'bru bzhi pa'o//baM khraM dza taM/yon tan gyi 'bru bzhi pa'o//khru khra ka
627-9(di): suM/'phrin las kyi 'bru bzhi pa'o//pe laM tsu khraM/brnag pa'i 'bru bzhi pa'o//
627-10(di): khro ru ka thaM/bya khyung gi 'bru bzhi pa'o//khri pas khriM/bya khyad gsung gi'o//
627-11(di): ha pa khri khro/bya khyad thugs kyi'o//'bru bzhi 'di dag ye shes lha yi snying po
627-12(di): yin//bris nas lus la btags na go char 'gyur//gser ram dnula lam
627-13(di): rin chen be D+ur+a//sbrul shun la sogs de yi bri bar bshad//sa ma ya bnag
627-14(di): pa na ra skan/brnag pa snying la dgongs pa yi ge 'bru bzhi'i skabs te zher
627-15(di): gtad khram nag po gsang ba'i gzer//gti mug las gyur gdol pa
627-16(di): gsum//gnyan ni nam mkha' dag la gnas//sa bdag ri dang sa gzhi la/
628-1(di): klu ni chu la gnas pa yin//gnyan po chu dang shing la'ang gnas//chu mig lu
628-2(di): mar 'dug pa las//gos sngon pra mo'i rigs dag gnas//chu mig chu ma shor
628-3(di): ba la//gdug byed nag po lkug pa gnas//sa bdag nu ba bltas chu mig na//
628-4(di): nA ga nag po bram ze gnas//nub byang bltas pa'i chu mig na/btsan dang
628-5(di): bram ze mo'i rigs gnas//byang shar bltas pa'i chu mig na//dmangs rigs
628-6(di): nag po gnas pa yin//lto na kryu ni ser po gnas//sa gzhi nag po sbrul can
628-7(di): na//nag po mgo dgu gnas pa yin//sa dmar snum bag can da ga na/
628-8(di): dmar po lhag pa zhes pa gnas//ser po snum bag can de na//bram ze lcug
628-9(di): pa bya ba gnas//brag sna 'dor dang ba tsha can//ro skams mtshe na klu btsan
628-10(di): gnas//chu mig dang ni mig yin no//'dom do pa ni snying khar 'dod//
628-11(di): gsum pa lte ba yin par bshad//dong nas 'dom gang kha yin no//chu mig
628-12(di): sa phyogs dben pa gnod sbyin gnas//gza' sbyang chen po bsgrub par bya/
628-13(di): 'bras chen rtsi sman 'o ma dang //ti la dang ra khrag rtsi tas mchod//srog gi
628-14(di): snying po 'di dag go//oM pa shi ma gi tu tu ma ti ki dza la te sa ma ya dz+yA dzA/
628-15(di): rtags rnams byung nas gsung ba tsam/khyab 'jug chen po kun tu rgyug
628-16(di): 'jigs byed chen po sprul pa ni//lus po gcig la mgo bo dgu//mgo lus
628-17(di): thams cad mig gis khengs//sku mdog brjid pa'i skrag nag la//phyag g.yas
628-18(di): chu srin zhags pa thogs//g.yon pa sbrul gyi zhags pa thogs//pa ni srin la ma
628-19(di): ni gru//kru srin 'tshos pa'i rdzu 'phrul can//ming brgya po la mkhan stong po/
629-1(di): dus tshod tshes grangs kun gyi bdag/bar snang khams na gnas pa'i tshe/
629-2(di): 'jig rten khams rnams 'khrug par byed//nyi ma zla ba zas su za//rgya mtsho'i
629-3(di): gting na gnas pa'i tshe//kru rgyal ma lus 'khrug cing bkrag/thog dang ser
629-4(di): ba skar mda' stong //'jigs byed rog ti mi bsgrubs pa//dam rdzas gtor
629-5(di): ma 'bul lags kyis//sdang ba'i bgegs dang dgra bo la//glo bur nad dang
629-6(di): ki kang pho ba/de skad bskul la bsad pa brtsam//oM kha sha ba ri thun ri la/
629-7(di): me kha bra du ni lcags/a la se lang kri ti thugs rbad ya/gza'i khram shod
629-8(di): de nas yam shud dmar po la//'bru skyems bshos bus mchod//lug
629-9(di): kyang brgyan du brtsal byas la//bshos bu ri rab 'dra bar brtag/haM shaM tri
629-10(di): dam btsal tri ra gam sa ma ya dza dza/des ni srog bdag myur du 'ong //kye
629-11(di): 'jig rten 'das dang ma 'dasa mtshams shed nas//bdud dang gshin rje de na
629-12(di): gnas//gzhal yas dmar po'i bse mkhar nas//mi reng dmar po mdung
629-13(di): tsam cig/chibs su bya khyad sgrag po chebs//phyag na rgyal mtshan ru mtshon
629-14(di): 'phyar//sprul pa mi dmar rta dmar 'gyed//mangag gzhug 'gong po dpun
629-15(di): dgu 'gyed//sgrol ging chen por dam bcas pa//'bru'i gtor ma chang dang
629-16(di): ni//dam rdzas sbyan gzigs sna tshogs 'bul//gsol lo yam shud dmar po
629-17(di): mchod//tri kha thun b+h+ya/tri kha thun b+h+yo/ma ra ya thig thig b+h+yo/zhes pas
629-18(di): rzbad pa'i las bya'o//srog bdag dmar pos bsad pa'i skabs te so gcig
630-1(di): gtad khram gsang ba'i las cig la/thod chen nang du ming rus gzhug
630-2(di): thal mdag dag gis kha gcad la//drag po'i sngags 'di gdab par bya//sha
630-3(di): la rdza mo chum/sha la rdza mo thun/sha la rdza mo nan/de yi dmod pa 'di yin
630-4(di): no//sdig can khyod kyis ci ma byas//ri raba chen po'i 'og tu gzhug
630-5(di): 'khor lo chen po'i rtsibs la bskyon//rgya mtsho chen po'i gting du bsgyur/
630-6(di): ces brjod la mnan pa bya'o//klu'i dmod pa nag po skabs te so gnyis
630-7(di): yang ni gtad khram las dag la//thod par ming rus bcug pa la//khrag
630-8(di): dang sol phyes sngags 'di gdab//staM na ga po sod/mA ra ya thrim so de mA ra
630-9(di): ya thri ya sod/tsakra du zad/tsakra du tri zad/tsakra du tri sod/sha la rnzam
630-10(di): mo chum/sha la rdza mo nan/glang dmar thod pa'am zod chen mnan/gru mgo
630-11(di): ma ta kaM tsa kyi shu rtsa la/sha sha sha Thu ti nye ba re bu wa hu wa sa ta/bu'i rkyal
630-12(di): po bya khyan ga cu'i gsang sngags kyi skabs te so bzhi pa'o//
630-13(di): ro ma gtad kyi rgyad ces bya ba las kyi rim pa bstan pa/dgos pa'i yi ge
630-14(di): bstan pa rin po che'i bsrungs ma/gsang ba'i don bstan pa kru'i rgyal po gsang
630-15(di): ba'i lung /rgya gar gyi mkhas pa ka ma la shI la dang /pad+ma 'byung gnas kyis
